`plugin/ui.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "ui.h"
#include "resource.h"
#include <commctrl.h>
#include <shobjidl.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <filesystem>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

namespace vreui {
namespace {
HINSTANCE moduleInstance=nullptr;
HWND ownerWindow=nullptr,dialogWindow=nullptr,tooltipWindow=nullptr;
IFileOpenDialog* activePicker=nullptr;
Callbacks actions;
ViewState state;
bool applying=false;
std::wstring lastOutput;
constexpr size_t noCandidate=(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max)();
constexpr wchar_t scanHelp[]=L"Run until unpacking finishes, then scan. A running target is paused for capture and left paused. Select the EXE or DLL that owns the resources. A valid table alone cannot prove that unpacking has finished.";
constexpr wchar_t outputHelp[]=L"Resources are written to a new folder with manifest.json. Existing folders are never overwritten. Browse selects a parent folder and suggests an unused child folder.";

std::wstring text(int id) {
    HWND control=GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,id);
    const int count=GetWindowTextLengthW(control);
    std::wstring value(static_cast<size_t>(count)+1,L'\0');
    const int copied=GetWindowTextW(control,value.data(),count+1);
    value.resize(static_cast<size_t>((std::max)(copied,0)));
    return value;
}
uint64_t selectedModule() {
    const LRESULT row=SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_MODULE,CB_GETCURSEL,0,0);
    return row>=0 && static_cast<size_t>(row)<state.modules.size()?state.modules[static_cast<size_t>(row)].base:0;
}
size_t selectedCandidate() {
    const LRESULT row=SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_TABLE,CB_GETCURSEL,0,0);
    return row>=0 && static_cast<size_t>(row)<state.candidates.size()?state.candidates[static_cast<size_t>(row)].index:noCandidate;
}
void enableControls() {
    const bool idle=!state.busy;
    for(int id:{IDC_VRE_MODULE,IDC_VRE_REFRESH,IDC_VRE_OUTPUT,IDC_VRE_BROWSE})
        EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,id),idle);
    EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_TABLE),idle&&!state.candidates.empty());
    EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_SCAN),idle&&selectedModule()!=0);
    EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_EXTRACT),idle&&selectedModule()!=0&&selectedCandidate()!=noCandidate&&!text(IDC_VRE_OUTPUT).empty());
}
template<class T> bool sameChoices(const std::vector<T>& a,const std::vector<T>& b) {
    if(a.size()!=b.size()) return false;
    for(size_t i=0;i<a.size();++i) {
        if(a[i].label!=b[i].label) return false;
        if constexpr(std::is_same_v<T,ModuleChoice>) { if(a[i].base!=b[i].base) return false; }
        else { if(a[i].index!=b[i].index) return false; }
    }
    return true;
}
void render(const ViewState& next,bool initial=false) {
    const uint64_t oldModule=initial?0:selectedModule();
    const size_t oldCandidate=initial?noCandidate:selectedCandidate();
    const bool modulesChanged=initial||!sameChoices(state.modules,next.modules);
    const bool moduleChanged=next.selectedModule&&next.selectedModule!=oldModule;
    const bool candidatesChanged=initial||moduleChanged||!sameChoices(state.candidates,next.candidates);
    applying=true;
    state=next;
    if(modulesChanged) {
        SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_MODULE,CB_RESETCONTENT,0,0);
        for(const auto& choice:state.modules)
            SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_MODULE,CB_ADDSTRING,0,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(choice.label.c_str()));
    }
    if(modulesChanged||state.selectedModule) {
        const uint64_t wanted=state.selectedModule?state.selectedModule:oldModule;
        size_t row=0;
        for(size_t i=0;i<state.modules.size();++i) if(state.modules[i].base==wanted) { row=i;break; }
        SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_MODULE,CB_SETCURSEL,state.modules.empty()?static_cast<WPARAM>(-1):row,0);
    }
    if(candidatesChanged) {
        SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_TABLE,CB_RESETCONTENT,0,0);
        for(const auto& choice:state.candidates)
            SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_TABLE,CB_ADDSTRING,0,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(choice.label.c_str()));
    }
    if(candidatesChanged||state.selectedCandidate!=noCandidate) {
        // A new scan with no explicit selection must not silently choose the
        // first of several valid tables. Ordinary progress updates preserve a
        // selection the user already made in an unchanged list.
        const size_t wanted=state.selectedCandidate!=noCandidate?state.selectedCandidate:
            (candidatesChanged?noCandidate:oldCandidate);
        WPARAM row=static_cast<WPARAM>(-1);
        if(wanted!=noCandidate) {
            for(size_t i=0;i<state.candidates.size();++i)
                if(state.candidates[i].index==wanted) { row=i;break; }
        }
        SendDlgItemMessageW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_TABLE,CB_SETCURSEL,row,0);
    }
    if(initial||state.output!=lastOutput) {
        SetDlgItemTextW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_OUTPUT,state.output.c_str());
        lastOutput=state.output;
    }
    SetDlgItemTextW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_STATUS,state.status.c_str());
    applying=false;
    enableControls();
}
void addTip(int id,const wchar_t* value) {
    TOOLINFOW tool{};
    tool.cbSize=sizeof(tool); tool.uFlags=TTF_IDISHWND|TTF_SUBCLASS;
    tool.hwnd=dialogWindow; tool.uId=reinterpret_cast<UINT_PTR>(GetDlgItem(dialogWindow,id));
    tool.lpszText=const_cast<wchar_t*>(value);
    SendMessageW(tooltipWindow,TTM_ADDTOOLW,0,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(&tool));
}
void createTips() {
    tooltipWindow=CreateWindowExW(WS_EX_TOPMOST|WS_EX_NOACTIVATE,TOOLTIPS_CLASSW,nullptr,
        WS_POPUP|TTS_ALWAYSTIP|TTS_NOPREFIX,CW_USEDEFAULT,CW_USEDEFAULT,CW_USEDEFAULT,CW_USEDEFAULT,
        dialogWindow,nullptr,moduleInstance,nullptr);
    if(!tooltipWindow) return;
    SendMessageW(tooltipWindow,WM_SETFONT,SendMessageW(dialogWindow,WM_GETFONT,0,0),FALSE);
    SendMessageW(tooltipWindow,TTM_SETMAXTIPWIDTH,0,MulDiv(350,GetDpiForWindow(dialogWindow),96));
    SendMessageW(tooltipWindow,TTM_SETDELAYTIME,TTDT_INITIAL,300);
    SendMessageW(tooltipWindow,TTM_SETDELAYTIME,TTDT_AUTOPOP,20000);
    for(int id:{IDC_VRE_HELP_SCAN,IDC_VRE_SCAN,IDC_VRE_TABLE}) addTip(id,scanHelp);
    for(int id:{IDC_VRE_HELP_OUTPUT,IDC_VRE_OUTPUT,IDC_VRE_BROWSE}) addTip(id,outputHelp);
    addTip(IDC_VRE_MODULE,L"Select the main EXE or a loaded DLL. Refresh updates the list of loaded modules.");
    addTip(IDC_VRE_STATUS,LPSTR_TEXTCALLBACKW);
}
void browse() {
    const HRESULT apartment=CoInitializeEx(nullptr,COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED);
    IFileOpenDialog* picker=nullptr;
    HRESULT result=CoCreateInstance(CLSID_FileOpenDialog,nullptr,CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER,IID_PPV_ARGS(&picker));
    if(SUCCEEDED(result)) {
        DWORD flags=0;
        result=picker->GetOptions(&flags);
        if(SUCCEEDED(result)) result=picker->SetOptions(flags|FOS_PICKFOLDERS|FOS_FORCEFILESYSTEM|FOS_PATHMUSTEXIST);
        if(SUCCEEDED(result)) result=picker->SetTitle(L"Choose an output parent folder");
        if(SUCCEEDED(result)) {
            activePicker=picker;
            result=picker->Show(dialogWindow);
            activePicker=nullptr;
        }
        if(SUCCEEDED(result)) {
            IShellItem* item=nullptr;
            result=picker->GetResult(&item);
            if(SUCCEEDED(result)) {
                PWSTR folder=nullptr;
                result=item->GetDisplayName(SIGDN_FILESYSPATH,&folder);
                if(SUCCEEDED(result)) {
                    try {
                        std::filesystem::path leaf=std::filesystem::path(text(IDC_VRE_OUTPUT)).filename();
                        if(leaf.empty()||leaf==L"."||leaf==L"..") leaf=L"extracted-resources";
                        std::filesystem::path target=std::filesystem::path(folder)/leaf;
                        const auto original=target;
                        unsigned suffix=2;
                        while(std::filesystem::exists(target)&&suffix<10000)
                            target=std::filesystem::path(original.wstring()+L"-"+std::to_wstring(suffix++));
                        if(std::filesystem::exists(target)) throw std::runtime_error("No unused output name");
                        SetDlgItemTextW(dialogWindow,IDC_VRE_OUTPUT,target.c_str());
                    } catch(const std::exception&) {
                        MessageBoxW(dialogWindow,L"Could not select a new output folder. Enter a new folder path manually.",L"VMProtect Resource Extractor",MB_OK|MB_ICONERROR);
                    }
                    CoTaskMemFree(folder);
                }
                item->Release();
            }
        }
        picker->Release();
    }
    if(SUCCEEDED(apartment)) CoUninitialize();
    if(FAILED(result)&&result!=HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_CANCELLED))
        MessageBoxW(dialogWindow,L"The folder browser could not open. Enter the output folder path manually.",L"VMProtect Resource Extractor",MB_OK|MB_ICONERROR);
}
INT_PTR CALLBACK dialogProc(HWND wnd,UINT message,WPARAM wp,LPARAM lp) {
    if(message==WM_INITDIALOG) {
        dialogWindow=wnd;
        SendMessageW(wnd,WM_SETICON,ICON_SMALL,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(LoadIconW(nullptr,IDI_APPLICATION)));
        SendMessageW(wnd,WM_SETICON,ICON_BIG,reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(LoadIconW(nullptr,IDI_APPLICATION)));
        SendDlgItemMessageW(wnd,IDC_VRE_OUTPUT,EM_SETLIMITTEXT,32767,0);
        const ViewState initial=state;
        render(initial,true); createTips();
        return TRUE;
    }
    if(message==WM_COMMAND&&!applying) {
        const int id=LOWORD(wp),notification=HIWORD(wp);
        if(id==IDC_VRE_MODULE&&notification==CBN_SELCHANGE) {
            const uint64_t base=selectedModule();
            state.selectedModule=base;
            state.candidates.clear();state.selectedCandidate=noCandidate;
            SendDlgItemMessageW(wnd,IDC_VRE_TABLE,CB_RESETCONTENT,0,0);
            state.status=L"Scan the selected module to find its protected resources.";
            SetDlgItemTextW(wnd,IDC_VRE_STATUS,state.status.c_str()); enableControls();
            if(actions.moduleChanged) actions.moduleChanged(base);
            return TRUE;
        }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_TABLE&&notification==CBN_SELCHANGE) { state.selectedCandidate=selectedCandidate();enableControls();return TRUE; }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_OUTPUT&&notification==EN_CHANGE) {
            state.output=text(IDC_VRE_OUTPUT);enableControls();
            if(actions.outputChanged) actions.outputChanged(state.output);
            return TRUE;
        }
        if(id==IDCANCEL) { DestroyWindow(wnd);return TRUE; }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_ABOUT) {
            MessageBoxW(wnd,L"VMProtect Resource Extractor\n\nCreator: Ivan Kozlovsky\ncontact@ivankozlovsky.com\nivankozlovsky.com\n\nExtract protected resources from a paused EXE or DLL.",L"About VMProtect Resource Extractor",MB_OK|MB_ICONINFORMATION);
            return TRUE;
        }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_HELP_SCAN||id==IDC_VRE_HELP_OUTPUT) {
            MessageBoxW(wnd,id==IDC_VRE_HELP_SCAN?scanHelp:outputHelp,L"VMProtect Resource Extractor",MB_OK|MB_ICONINFORMATION);return TRUE;
        }
        if(state.busy) return FALSE;
        if(id==IDC_VRE_BROWSE) { browse();return TRUE; }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_REFRESH) { if(actions.refresh) actions.refresh();return TRUE; }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_SCAN) { const auto base=selectedModule();if(base&&actions.scan) actions.scan(base);return TRUE; }
        if(id==IDC_VRE_EXTRACT) {
            const auto base=selectedModule();const auto candidate=selectedCandidate();
            const auto output=text(IDC_VRE_OUTPUT);
            if(base&&candidate!=noCandidate&&!output.empty()&&actions.extract) actions.extract(base,candidate,output);
            return TRUE;
        }
    }
    if(message==WM_NOTIFY) {
        auto* header=reinterpret_cast<NMHDR*>(lp);
        if(header&&header->code==TTN_GETDISPINFOW) {
            auto* tip=reinterpret_cast<NMTTDISPINFOW*>(lp);
            tip->lpszText=const_cast<wchar_t*>(state.status.c_str());return TRUE;
        }
    }
    if(message==WM_CTLCOLORSTATIC) {
        const int id=GetDlgCtrlID(reinterpret_cast<HWND>(lp));
        if(id==IDC_VRE_HELP_SCAN||id==IDC_VRE_HELP_OUTPUT) {
            SetTextColor(reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp),RGB(0,72,130));SetBkMode(reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp),TRANSPARENT);
            return reinterpret_cast<INT_PTR>(GetSysColorBrush(COLOR_3DFACE));
        }
    }
    if(message==WM_CLOSE) { DestroyWindow(wnd);return TRUE; }
    if(message==WM_DESTROY) {
        if(tooltipWindow) { DestroyWindow(tooltipWindow);tooltipWindow=nullptr; }
        dialogWindow=nullptr;return TRUE;
    }
    return FALSE;
}
}

void initialize(HINSTANCE instance,HWND owner,Callbacks callbacks) {
    moduleInstance=instance;ownerWindow=owner;actions=std::move(callbacks);
    INITCOMMONCONTROLSEX controls{sizeof(controls),ICC_STANDARD_CLASSES};
    InitCommonControlsEx(&controls);
}
bool show() {
    if(dialogWindow) { ShowWindow(dialogWindow,SW_RESTORE);SetForegroundWindow(dialogWindow);return true; }
    HWND created=CreateDialogParamW(moduleInstance,MAKEINTRESOURCEW(IDD_VRE),ownerWindow,dialogProc,0);
    if(!created) return false;
    ShowWindow(created,SW_SHOW);SetForegroundWindow(created);return true;
}
void close() {
    if(activePicker) activePicker->Close(HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_CANCELLED));
    const HWND parent=dialogWindow;
    if(parent) {
        EnumThreadWindows(GetCurrentThreadId(),[](HWND wnd,LPARAM owner)->BOOL {
            if(GetWindow(wnd,GW_OWNER)==reinterpret_cast<HWND>(owner)) SendMessageW(wnd,WM_CLOSE,0,0);
            return TRUE;
        },reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(parent));
        if(IsWindow(parent)) DestroyWindow(parent);
    }
}
void update(const ViewState& next) { if(dialogWindow) render(next);else state=next; }
bool is_open() { return dialogWindow!=nullptr; }
HWND window() { return dialogWindow; }
bool translate(MSG* message) {
    return message&&dialogWindow&&(message->hwnd==dialogWindow||IsChild(dialogWindow,message->hwnd))&&IsDialogMessageW(dialogWindow,message)!=FALSE;
}
}
```

```cmd
@echo off
rem Called by build scripts inside their setlocal scope. Keep the toolchain
rem environment available to the caller.
if /i "%VSCMD_ARG_TGT_ARCH%"=="x64" (
  where cl >nul 2>nul
  if not errorlevel 1 exit /b 0
)

set "VRE_VSWHERE=%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe"
if not exist "%VRE_VSWHERE%" (
  echo Visual Studio C++ build tools were not found. Install the Desktop development with C++ workload, or run from an x64 Native Tools command prompt.
  exit /b 1
)
set "VRE_VSROOT="
for /f "usebackq delims=" %%I in (`"%VRE_VSWHERE%" -latest -products * -requires Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64 -property installationPath`) do set "VRE_VSROOT=%%I"
if not defined VRE_VSROOT (
  echo No Visual Studio installation with the x64 C++ toolchain was found.
  exit /b 1
)
if not exist "%VRE_VSROOT%\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" (
  echo The selected Visual Studio installation has no vcvars64.bat.
  exit /b 1
)
call "%VRE_VSROOT%\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" >nul
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
where cl >nul 2>nul
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo The x64 C++ compiler could not be initialized.
  exit /b 1
)
exit /b 0
```

`scripts/package-release.py`:

```py
"""Package existing Windows x64 builds without running protected inputs."""
import argparse
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import re
import struct
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPO = 'https://github.com/ivanpotatoes/VMProtect-Resource-Extractor'
SOURCES = {
    'unicorn-2.1.4.tar.gz': '00567a70e323f749b419cd86bee4f9115beab7ebba32194581c090cbb7c59cff',
    'pyinstaller-6.22.3.tar.gz': '05eb2f5615503e72939a7224d68b4aff572c6b0438ee4a17d0a4b481f399362d',
}


def executable(name):
    path = ROOT / 'dist' / name
    data = path.read_bytes()
    if data[:2] != b'MZ' or len(data) < 64:
        raise ValueError(f'Not a PE binary: {path}')
    nt = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, 0x3c)[0]
    if data[nt:nt + 4] != b'PE\0\0' or struct.unpack_from('<H', data, nt + 4)[0] != 0x8664:
        raise ValueError(f'Not an x64 PE binary: {path}')
    return data


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--version', required=True, help='Release version, e.g. 1.0.0')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not re.fullmatch(r'\d+\.\d+\.\d+', args.version):
        parser.error('Use a three-part numeric version')
    version = args.version
    title = f'VMProtect Resource Extractor v{version}'
    footer = f'\nSource and documentation: {REPO}/tree/v{version}\n\nIvan Kozlovsky\nivankozlovsky.com\ncontact@ivankozlovsky.com\n'
    static_readme = f'''{title} - Static CLI

Extract this ZIP, then run from a command prompt:
  vmp-resource-extractor.exe protected.exe
  vmp-resource-extractor.exe protected.dll --output extracted
  vmp-resource-extractor.exe protected.exe --list

Windows x64. No Python, debugger or driver required.
Output: numbered raw resource files and manifest.json.
Use a new output folder beneath an existing parent directory.
The input file is unchanged. This does not rebuild .rsrc.
If packing hides the resource table or payloads, use the emulator
or scan the initialized module with the x64dbg plugin.
''' + footer
    emulator_readme = f'''{title} - Emulation CLI

Extract this ZIP, then run:
  vmp-resource-emulator.exe protected.exe --output extracted
  vmp-resource-emulator.exe protected.dll --seconds 600 --instructions 15000000000

Windows x64. Python, Unicorn and Capstone are bundled.
Protected startup code runs inside Unicorn, not as a Windows process.
Default budgets: 240 seconds and five billion instructions.
Unsupported APIs, exceptions and exhausted budgets stop with an error.
This models tested startup paths, not a complete Windows environment.

Output: numbered raw resources and manifest.json. Existing output
folders are never overwritten. The input file is unchanged.
The static extractor is also included for direct extraction.

Third-party license texts and source links are included in this ZIP.
''' + footer
    common = {'LICENSE': (ROOT / 'LICENSE').read_bytes()}
    packages = {
        'static': {**common, 'vmp-resource-extractor.exe': executable('vmp-resource-extractor.exe'),
                   'README.txt': static_readme.encode('utf-8')},
        'emulation': {**common, 'vmp-resource-emulator.exe': executable('vmp-resource-emulator.exe'),
                      'vmp-resource-extractor.exe': executable('vmp-resource-extractor.exe'),
                      'README.txt': emulator_readme.encode('utf-8'),
                      'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md': (ROOT / 'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md').read_bytes(),
                      **{f'licenses/{p.name}': p.read_bytes() for p in sorted((ROOT / 'licenses').glob('*.txt'))}},
        'x64dbg': {**common, 'VMPResourceExtractor.dp64': executable('VMPResourceExtractor.dp64'),
                   'README.txt': (ROOT / 'plugin/README.txt').read_bytes()},
    }
    source_files = {}
    for name, expected in SOURCES.items():
        data = (ROOT / 'build/vendor' / name).read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != expected:
            raise ValueError(f'Dependency source hash mismatch: {name}')
        source_files[name] = data
    source_files['SHA256SUMS.txt'] = ''.join(f'{digest}  {name}\n' for name, digest in SOURCES.items()).encode('ascii')
    source_files['README.txt'] = f'''{title} - Emulation dependency sources

Unmodified upstream source archives matching the bundled Unicorn 2.1.4
and PyInstaller 6.22.3 components. Their license texts are in the archives.
The archive hashes were checked against the official PyPI release metadata.

Unicorn source and build instructions: https://github.com/unicorn-engine/unicorn/tree/2.1.4
PyInstaller source: https://github.com/pyinstaller/pyinstaller/tree/v6.22.3
The PyInstaller bootloader is built with: py waf all --no-cfg

Project code and full build scripts: {REPO}/tree/v{version}
See build-bootloader.cmd, build-emulation.cmd and docs/BUILDING.md.
'''.encode('utf-8')
    packages['emulation-sources'] = source_files
    output = ROOT / 'dist/releases' / f'v{version}'
    if output.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(f'Release directory already exists: {output}')
    output.mkdir(parents=True)
    hashes = []
    for mode, files in packages.items():
        suffix = '' if mode == 'emulation-sources' else '-x64'
        path = output / f'VMProtect-Resource-Extractor-v{version}-{mode}{suffix}.zip'
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path, 'x', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=9) as archive:
            for name, data in sorted(files.items()):
                archive.writestr(name, data)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
            if archive.testzip() is not None:
                raise ValueError(f'Invalid ZIP: {path}')
            for name, data in files.items():
                if archive.read(name) != data:
                    raise ValueError(f'Packaging mismatch: {path}/{name}')
        hashes.append(f'{hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {path.name}')
        print(f'{path.name}: {path.stat().st_size} bytes, {len(files)} files')
    (output / 'SHA256SUMS.txt').write_text('\n'.join(hashes) + '\n', encoding='ascii')
    print(f'Created {output}')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`standalone/block_budget.cpp`:

```cpp
// Optional synchronous Unicorn scheduler. Never changes guest registers/memory.
#define NOMINMAX
#include <windows.h>
#include <capstone/capstone.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <filesystem>
#include <memory>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#define API extern "C" __declspec(dllexport)
using ReadFn=int(*)(void*,uint64_t,void*,size_t);
using StopFn=int(*)(void*);
enum Reason : uint32_t { RUNNING=0, SLICE=1, TOTAL=2, DEADLINE=3, BAD_BLOCK=4, READ_ERROR=5, DECODE_ERROR=6, HOST_ERROR=7 };
struct Key {uint64_t address; uint32_t size; bool operator==(const Key& k) const {return address==k.address&&size==k.size;}};
struct Hash {size_t operator()(const Key& k) const {return size_t(k.address^(uint64_t(k.size)<<48));}};
struct Entry {std::vector<uint8_t> bytes; uint32_t instructions;};
struct Budget {
    HMODULE dll=nullptr;
    decltype(&cs_open) open=nullptr; decltype(&cs_close) close=nullptr;
    decltype(&cs_version) version=nullptr;
    decltype(&cs_malloc) allocate=nullptr; decltype(&cs_free) free=nullptr;
    decltype(&cs_disasm_iter) decode=nullptr;
    csh handle=0; cs_insn* instruction=nullptr;
    ReadFn read=nullptr; StopFn stop=nullptr;
    const uint8_t* image=nullptr; uint64_t image_base=0,image_size=0;
    uint64_t total_limit=0,total=0,slice_limit=0,slice=0,blocks=0,decodes=0,hits=0;
    LONGLONG deadline=0,frequency=0;
    uint32_t reason=RUNNING;
    size_t bytes=0;
    std::unordered_map<Key,Entry,Hash> cache;
    ~Budget(){if(instruction&&free)free(instruction,1); if(handle&&close)close(&handle); if(dll)FreeLibrary(dll);}
    void halt(void* uc,Reason r){reason=r;stop(uc);}
};
template<class T> T symbol(HMODULE dll,const char* name){return reinterpret_cast<T>(GetProcAddress(dll,name));}

API void* bb_create(const wchar_t* capstone,void* read,void* stop,uint64_t limit) noexcept {
    if(!capstone||!read||!stop||!limit)return nullptr;
    try {
        auto p=std::make_unique<Budget>();
        if(!std::filesystem::path(capstone).is_absolute())return nullptr;
        p->dll=LoadLibraryExW(capstone,nullptr,LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR|LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DEFAULT_DIRS);
        if(!p->dll)return nullptr;
        p->version=symbol<decltype(p->version)>(p->dll,"cs_version");
        if(!p->version)return nullptr;
        int major=0,minor=0;p->version(&major,&minor);
        if(major!=CS_API_MAJOR)return nullptr;
        p->open=symbol<decltype(p->open)>(p->dll,"cs_open");
        p->close=symbol<decltype(p->close)>(p->dll,"cs_close");
        p->allocate=symbol<decltype(p->allocate)>(p->dll,"cs_malloc");
        p->free=symbol<decltype(p->free)>(p->dll,"cs_free");
        p->decode=symbol<decltype(p->decode)>(p->dll,"cs_disasm_iter");
        if(!p->open||!p->close||!p->allocate||!p->free||!p->decode)return nullptr;
        if(p->open(CS_ARCH_X86,CS_MODE_64,&p->handle)!=CS_ERR_OK)return nullptr;
        p->instruction=p->allocate(p->handle); if(!p->instruction)return nullptr;
        p->read=reinterpret_cast<ReadFn>(read); p->stop=reinterpret_cast<StopFn>(stop); p->total_limit=limit;
        LARGE_INTEGER f; QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f); p->frequency=f.QuadPart;
        return p.release();
    }catch(...){return nullptr;}
}
API void bb_destroy(void* state) noexcept {delete static_cast<Budget*>(state);}
API int bb_set_image(void* state,uint64_t base,uint64_t size,void* pointer) noexcept {
    auto p=static_cast<Budget*>(state); if(!p)return 0;
    // Borrowed storage must remain the backing of the same Unicorn mapping until
    // cleared or destroyed. Configure only while emulation is stopped.
    if(!pointer&&!base&&!size){p->image=nullptr;p->image_base=0;p->image_size=0;return 1;}
    const auto host=reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(pointer);
    if(!pointer||!size||size>SIZE_MAX||size-1>UINT64_MAX-base||size-1>UINTPTR_MAX-host)return 0;
    p->image=static_cast<const uint8_t*>(pointer);p->image_base=base;p->image_size=size;return 1;
}
API int bb_begin(void* state,uint64_t slice,uint32_t milliseconds) noexcept {
    auto p=static_cast<Budget*>(state); if(!p||!slice||!milliseconds)return 0;
    LARGE_INTEGER now; QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
    p->deadline=now.QuadPart+(p->frequency*milliseconds)/1000;
    p->slice_limit=slice;p->slice=0;p->reason=RUNNING;return 1;
}
API uint32_t bb_reason(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->reason:HOST_ERROR;}
API uint64_t bb_total(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->total:0;}
API uint64_t bb_slice(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->slice:0;}
API uint64_t bb_blocks(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->blocks:0;}
API uint64_t bb_decodes(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->decodes:0;}
API uint64_t bb_hits(void* state) noexcept {return state?static_cast<Budget*>(state)->hits:0;}

API void bb_callback(void* uc,uint64_t address,uint32_t size,void* state) noexcept {
    auto p=static_cast<Budget*>(state); if(!p)return;
    try {
        if(p->reason!=RUNNING){p->stop(uc);return;}
        // A terminal budget condition is checked before reading/decoding a block.
        if(p->total>=p->total_limit){p->halt(uc,TOTAL);return;}
        if(p->slice>=p->slice_limit){p->halt(uc,SLICE);return;}
        // Synchronous coarse checks avoid a host clock query on every hot block.
        // The caller retains an absolute Unicorn timeout as emergency protection.
        if(!p->slice || !(p->blocks&255)) {
            LARGE_INTEGER now; QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
            if(now.QuadPart>=p->deadline){p->halt(uc,DEADLINE);return;}
        }
        if(!size||size>16384){p->halt(uc,BAD_BLOCK);return;}
        uint8_t bytes[16384];
        if(p->image&&address>=p->image_base&&address-p->image_base<p->image_size&&
           size<=p->image_size-(address-p->image_base)) {
            std::memcpy(bytes,p->image+static_cast<size_t>(address-p->image_base),size);
        }else if(p->read(uc,address,bytes,size)){p->halt(uc,READ_ERROR);return;}
        Key key{address,size}; uint32_t count=0;
        auto hit=p->cache.find(key);
        if(hit!=p->cache.end()&&std::memcmp(hit->second.bytes.data(),bytes,size)==0){count=hit->second.instructions;++p->hits;}
        else {
            const uint8_t* cursor=bytes; size_t remaining=size; uint64_t pc=address;
            while(remaining){
                size_t before=remaining;
                if(!p->decode(p->handle,&cursor,&remaining,&pc,p->instruction)||remaining>=before){p->halt(uc,DECODE_ERROR);return;}
                ++count;
            }
            ++p->decodes;
            // Cache lifetime is bounded. Exact bytes are checked even on a cache hit.
            if(p->cache.size()>=32768||p->bytes+size>32u*1024*1024){p->cache.clear();p->bytes=0;}
            auto& entry=p->cache[key];
            p->bytes-=entry.bytes.size();entry.bytes.assign(bytes,bytes+size);p->bytes+=size;entry.instructions=count;
        }
        if(count>p->total_limit-p->total){p->halt(uc,TOTAL);return;}
        if(p->slice&&count>p->slice_limit-p->slice){p->halt(uc,SLICE);return;}
        // Charge the entire block before entering it. A code/API hook may stop
        // midway, so this is a conservative upper bound, not retired instructions.
        p->total+=count;p->slice+=count;++p->blocks;
    }catch(...){p->halt(uc,HOST_ERROR);}
}

```

`standalone/emulate.py`:

```py
"""Offline Unicorn execution harness. Guest code never calls host Windows APIs."""
import argparse
from collections import Counter, deque
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import zipfile
import hashlib
import sys
import os
import pefile
import unicorn as u
from unicorn import x86_const as x
import capstone
from native_support import Coverage, Scanner, BlockBudget, HostImage, library_path
from work_files import work_directory

def align(n): return (n + 4095) & ~4095
class Unsupported(RuntimeError): pass

def atomic_snapshot(path,image):
    path=Path(path)
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=path.parent,prefix=path.name+'.',suffix='.writing',delete=False) as f:
        temporary=Path(f.name)
        f.write(image)
    try:
        for attempt in range(20):
            try:
                os.replace(temporary,path)
                return
            except OSError:
                if attempt==19: raise
                time.sleep(0.1)
    finally:
        temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)

def scanner_path():
    if getattr(sys,'frozen',False): return Path(sys._MEIPASS)/'vmp-resource-extractor.exe'
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'dist/vmp-resource-extractor.exe'

def scan_run(*args):
    return subprocess.run([str(scanner_path()),*map(str,args)],capture_output=True,text=True,
                          encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=20,creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)

class Emulator:
    def __init__(self, path, reference=False):
        print('Loading PE image...',flush=True)
        self.path = Path(path)
        if not 64<=self.path.stat().st_size<=1024*1024*1024: raise Unsupported('Input size must be 64 bytes to 1 GiB')
        self.pe = pefile.PE(str(path))
        if self.pe.FILE_HEADER.Machine != 0x8664: raise Unsupported('Emulation currently requires x64')
        self.base = self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.ImageBase
        self.size = align(self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfImage)
        if not 0<self.size<=256*1024*1024: raise Unsupported('Image size must be 1 byte to 256 MiB')
        if self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfHeaders>self.size: raise Unsupported('Headers extend beyond image')
        mapped=self.pe.get_memory_mapped_image()
        if len(mapped)>self.size: raise Unsupported('Mapped data extends beyond image')
        self.uc = u.Uc(u.UC_ARCH_X86, u.UC_MODE_64)
        self.host_image=None if reference else HostImage(self.uc,self.base,self.size)
        if self.host_image: self.host_image.map_region(self.base,self.size)
        else: self.uc.mem_map(self.base,self.size)
        self.uc.mem_write(self.base,mapped)
        self.written=bytearray(self.size)
        self.written[:self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfHeaders]=b'\1'*self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfHeaders
        self.native_coverage=None
        self.scanner=None
        self.scan_hint=None
        self.metrics={'emulation_seconds':0.0,'scan_seconds':0.0,'snapshot_seconds':0.0,'scans':0}
        if not reference and library_path('vmp-native-tracker.dll').exists():
            self.native_coverage=Coverage(self.uc,self.base,self.written)
        if not reference and library_path('resource_scanner.dll').exists():
            self.scanner=Scanner()
        for section in self.pe.sections:
            start=section.VirtualAddress
            if start+max(section.SizeOfRawData,section.Misc_VirtualSize)>self.size:
                raise Unsupported('Section extends beyond image')
            if section.SizeOfRawData:
                self.written[start:start+section.SizeOfRawData]=b'\1'*section.SizeOfRawData
            elif section.Misc_VirtualSize:
                if self.native_coverage: self.native_coverage.attach(self.base+start,self.base+start+section.Misc_VirtualSize-1)
                else: self.uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_MEM_WRITE,self.image_write,begin=self.base+start,end=self.base+start+section.Misc_VirtualSize-1)
        self.stack=0x10000000; self.teb=0x70000000; self.peb=self.teb+0x2000
        self.uc.mem_map(self.stack,0x200000)
        self.uc.mem_map(self.teb,0x20000)
        self.uc.mem_map(0x7ffe0000,0x1000)
        self.uc.mem_write(0x7ffe0260,struct.pack('<I',10))
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_GS_BASE,self.teb)
        self.q(self.teb+0x08,self.stack+0x200000); self.q(self.teb+0x10,self.stack)
        self.q(self.teb+0x30,self.teb); self.q(self.teb+0x60,self.peb)
        self.q(self.teb+0x40,1000); self.q(self.teb+0x48,1001)
        self.q(self.peb+0x10,self.base); self.q(self.peb+0x18,self.peb+0x100)
        self.q(self.peb+0x30,self.teb+0x10000)
        self.uc.mem_write(self.teb+0x10070,struct.pack('<II',2,0))
        self.uc.mem_write(self.peb+0x118,struct.pack('<III',10,0,19045))
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RSP,self.stack+0x1fff00-8)
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RIP,self.base+self.pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.AddressOfEntryPoint)
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_EFLAGS,0x202)
        if self.pe.FILE_HEADER.Characteristics & 0x2000:
            self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RCX,self.base)
            self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RDX,1)  # DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH
            self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_R8,0)
        self.modules={}; self.apis={}; self.calls=Counter(); self.blocks=deque(maxlen=16)
        self.heap=0x40000000; self.reason=None; self.steps=0; self.allocations=0
        self.handles={}; self.syscalls={}
        self.events=[]
        self.thread_hidden=False; self.function_tables=[]
        self.next_handle=0x100
        self.last_api_context=None
        self.api_failed=False
        self.nonresumable_reason=None
        self.load_modules()
        print('Guest image and Windows API models ready.',flush=True)
        self.uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_MEM_INVALID,self.bad_memory)
        self.uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_INTR,self.interrupt)
        # Per-block Python tracing makes virtualized startup disproportionately slow.
        # API events and the final instruction are sufficient for normal diagnostics.
        self.uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_INSN,self.syscall,None,1,0,x.UC_X86_INS_SYSCALL)
        self.md=capstone.Cs(capstone.CS_ARCH_X86,capstone.CS_MODE_64)

    def q(self,a,v): self.uc.mem_write(a,struct.pack('<Q',v & 0xffffffffffffffff))
    def image_write(self,uc,access,address,size,value,data):
        offset=address-self.base
        if 0<=offset and offset+size<=self.size: self.written[offset:offset+size]=b'\1'*size
    def readq(self,a): return struct.unpack('<Q',self.uc.mem_read(a,8))[0]
    def reg(self,n): return self.uc.reg_read(getattr(x,'UC_X86_REG_'+n.upper()))
    def text(self,a,wide=False):
        out=bytearray(); size=2 if wide else 1
        for i in range(4096):
            b=bytes(self.uc.mem_read(a+i*size,size))
            if not any(b): return out.decode('utf-16-le' if wide else 'ascii',errors='replace')
            out.extend(b)
        raise Unsupported('Unterminated guest string')
    def alloc(self,size):
        size=align(max(size,1))
        if self.allocations+size>256*1024*1024: raise Unsupported('Guest allocation budget exceeded')
        a=self.heap; self.heap+=size+0x1000; self.allocations+=size
        self.uc.mem_map(a,size); return a
    def handle(self,value):
        h=self.next_handle; self.next_handle+=4; self.handles[h]=value; return h
    def load_modules(self):
        exports={}; forwards={}
        modules=['ntdll.dll','kernel32.dll','kernelbase.dll']
        imports={entry.dll.decode().lower() for entry in getattr(self.pe,'DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT',[])}
        if 'user32.dll' in imports: modules.append('user32.dll')
        next_base=0x180000000
        for name in modules:
            pe=pefile.PE(str(Path('C:/Windows/System32')/name))
            size=align(pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfImage)
            base=next_base
            while any(base<=end and begin<base+size for begin,end,_ in self.uc.mem_regions()):
                base+=0x10000000
            next_base=base+0x10000000
            self.uc.mem_map(base,size)
            self.uc.mem_write(base,pe.get_memory_mapped_image())
            self.modules[name]=base
            for e in pe.DIRECTORY_ENTRY_EXPORT.symbols:
                key=(name,e.name.decode() if e.name else '#'+str(e.ordinal))
                exports[key]=base+e.address
                if e.forwarder: forwards[key]=e.forwarder.decode()
                else: self.apis.setdefault(base+e.address,key[1])
                if name=='ntdll.dll' and key[1].startswith('Nt') and not e.forwarder:
                    stub=pe.get_data(e.address,8)
                    if stub[:4]==b'\x4c\x8b\xd1\xb8': self.syscalls[struct.unpack('<I',stub[4:])[0]]=key[1]
            self.uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_CODE,self.api,begin=base,end=base+pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfImage-1)
            pe.close()
        for key,target in forwards.items():
            module,symbol=target.rsplit('.',1)
            resolved=exports.get((module.lower()+'.dll',symbol))
            if resolved:
                # Forwarded export strings remain intact for guest export walkers.
                exports[key]=resolved
        self.exports=exports
        for imp in getattr(self.pe,'DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT',[]):
            for entry in imp.imports:
                key=(imp.dll.decode().lower(),entry.name.decode() if entry.name else '#'+str(entry.ordinal))
                if key not in exports: raise Unsupported('Unmodeled import '+str(key))
                self.q(entry.address,exports[key])
        # Minimal loader lists, including the image and mapped system modules.
        entries=[]
        for i,(name,base) in enumerate([(self.path.name,self.base),*self.modules.items()]):
            e=self.peb+0x400+i*0x200; entries.append(e)
            self.q(e+0x30,base)
            text=name.encode('utf-16-le'); ptr=e+0x100
            self.uc.mem_write(ptr,text+b'\0\0')
            for offset in [0x48,0x58]:
                self.uc.mem_write(e+offset,struct.pack('<HHIQ',len(text),len(text)+2,0,ptr))
        for offset,head_offset in [(0,0x10),(0x10,0x20),(0x20,0x30)]:
            head=self.peb+0x100+head_offset
            nodes=[head]+[e+offset for e in entries]
            for i,node in enumerate(nodes): self.q(node,nodes[(i+1)%len(nodes)]); self.q(node+8,nodes[i-1])
    def stop(self,reason): self.reason=reason; self.uc.emu_stop()
    def bad_memory(self,uc,access,address,size,value,data):
        self.stop(f'unmapped memory access={access} address={address:#x} size={size} rip={self.reg("rip"):#x}')
        return False
    def interrupt(self,uc,number,data): self.stop(f'unsupported CPU exception {number} at {self.reg("rip"):#x}')
    def block(self,uc,address,size,data): self.blocks.append(address)
    def ret(self,value):
        if getattr(self,'in_syscall',False):
            self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RAX,value & 0xffffffffffffffff); return
        sp=self.reg('rsp'); target=self.readq(sp)
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RAX,value & 0xffffffffffffffff)
        self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RSP,sp+8); self.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RIP,target)
        self.uc.emu_stop()
    def api(self,uc,address,size,data):
        self.last_api_context=self.uc.context_save()
        name=self.syscalls.get(self.reg('rax')) if getattr(self,'in_syscall',False) else self.apis.get(address)
        if not name: self.stop(f'unsupported system module instruction {address:#x}'); return
        self.calls[name]+=1
        a,b,c,d=[self.reg(n) for n in ['r10' if getattr(self,'in_syscall',False) else 'rcx','rdx','r8','r9']]
        self.events.append({'name':name,'args':[hex(v) for v in [a,b,c,d]]})
        try:
            if name in ['GetLastError','RtlGetLastWin32Error']: self.ret(0)
            elif name in ['RtlAllocateHeap','HeapAlloc']: self.ret(self.alloc(c))
            elif name in ['RtlFreeHeap','HeapFree']: self.ret(1)
            elif name=='GetProcessHeap': self.ret(self.readq(self.peb+0x30))
            elif name=='NtOpenSection':
                string=self.readq(c+16); label=self.text(self.readq(string+8),True)
                module=label.rsplit('\\',1)[-1].lower()
                if module not in self.modules: raise Unsupported('Section not modeled: '+label)
                self.q(a,self.handle(module)); self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtOpenFile':
                string=self.readq(c+16); label=self.text(self.readq(string+8),True)
                if label.lower() in ['\\device\\titanhide','\\??\\titanhide']:
                    self.q(a,0); self.uc.mem_write(d,struct.pack('<QQ',0xc0000034,0)); self.ret(0xc0000034)
                elif label.rsplit('\\',1)[-1].lower()==self.path.name.lower():
                    self.q(a,self.handle('input-file')); self.uc.mem_write(d,struct.pack('<QQ',0,1)); self.ret(0)
                else: raise Unsupported('File not modeled: '+label)
            elif name=='NtCreateSection':
                filehandle=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x38)
                if self.handles.get(filehandle)!='input-file': raise Unsupported('Section backing file not modeled')
                attributes=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x30)&0xffffffff
                self.q(a,self.handle('input-section' if attributes&0x1000000 else 'input-raw-section')); self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtMapViewOfSection':
                module=self.handles.get(a)
                if not module: raise Unsupported('Invalid section handle')
                if module=='input-raw-section': image=self.path.read_bytes()
                else:
                    pe=pefile.PE(str(self.path if module=='input-section' else Path('C:/Windows/System32')/module))
                    image=pe.get_memory_mapped_image(); pe.close()
                addr=self.alloc(len(image))
                self.uc.mem_write(addr,image); self.q(c,addr)
                sizeptr=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x38)
                if sizeptr: self.q(sizeptr,align(len(image)))
                self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtUnmapViewOfSection': self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtClose':
                self.ret(0 if self.handles.pop(a,None) is not None else 0xc0000008)
            elif name=='NtCreateDebugObject':
                self.q(a,self.handle('debug-object')); self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtReadFile':
                if self.handles.get(a)!='input-file': raise Unsupported('Read handle not modeled')
                sp=self.reg('rsp'); ios=self.readq(sp+0x28); buf=self.readq(sp+0x30)
                size=self.readq(sp+0x38)&0xffffffff; offsetptr=self.readq(sp+0x40)
                offset=self.readq(offsetptr) if offsetptr else 0
                blob=self.path.read_bytes()[offset:offset+size]
                self.uc.mem_write(buf,blob); self.uc.mem_write(ios,struct.pack('<QQ',0,len(blob))); self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtQueryObject':
                if self.handles.get(a)!='debug-object' or b!=2: raise Unsupported('Object query not modeled')
                length=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x28)
                if d<0x80 or not c:
                    if length: self.uc.mem_write(length,struct.pack('<I',0x80))
                    self.ret(0xc0000004)
                else:
                    value=bytearray(0x80); text='DebugObject'.encode('utf-16-le')
                    struct.pack_into('<HHIQII',value,0,len(text),len(text)+2,0,c+0x68,1,1)
                    value[0x68:0x68+len(text)]=text
                    self.uc.mem_write(c,bytes(value))
                    if length: self.uc.mem_write(length,struct.pack('<I',0x80))
                    self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtProtectVirtualMemory':
                old=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x28)
                self.uc.mem_write(old,struct.pack('<I',0x40)); self.ret(0)
            elif name=='NtSetInformationProcess':
                if b==40:
                    # ProcessInstrumentationCallback is not supported by this environment.
                    self.ret(0xc0000003)
                else: raise Unsupported(f'NtSetInformationProcess class {b}')
            elif name=='NtQueryInformationProcess':
                length=self.readq(self.reg('rsp')+0x28)
                if c&3: self.ret(0x80000002)
                elif length and length<0x10000: self.ret(0xc0000005)
                elif b in [7,30,31]:
                    needed=4 if b==31 else 8
                    if d!=needed: self.ret(0xc0000004)
                    else:
                        if b==7: self.q(c,0)
                        elif b==31: self.uc.mem_write(c,struct.pack('<I',1))
                        # Failed DebugObject queries leave the output unchanged.
                        # ReturnLength is written last, including overlapping buffers.
                        if length: self.uc.mem_write(length,struct.pack('<I',needed))
                        self.ret(0xc0000353 if b==30 else 0)
                else: raise Unsupported(f'NtQueryInformationProcess class {b}')
            elif name=='NtSetInformationThread':
                if b==17: self.thread_hidden=True; self.ret(0)
                else: raise Unsupported(f'NtSetInformationThread class {b}')
            elif name=='NtQueryInformationThread':
                if b==17 and d==1: self.uc.mem_write(c,bytes([self.thread_hidden])); self.ret(0)
                else: raise Unsupported(f'NtQueryInformationThread class {b}')
            elif name=='NtQuerySystemInformation':
                if a==35 and c>=2: self.uc.mem_write(b,b'\0\1'); self.ret(0)
                elif a==11 and c>=8: self.q(b,0); self.ret(0)
                else: raise Unsupported(f'NtQuerySystemInformation class {a}')
            elif name=='RtlAddFunctionTable': self.function_tables.append((a,b,c)); self.ret(1)
            elif name=='NtRaiseHardError':
                params=[self.readq(d+i*8) for i in range(min(b,8))]
                labels=[]
                for i,value in enumerate(params):
                    if c & (1<<i): labels.append(self.text(self.readq(value+8),True))
                raise Unsupported('Guest hard error: '+str(labels)+' status='+hex(a))
            elif name in ['GetModuleHandleW','GetModuleHandleA']:
                self.ret(self.modules.get(self.text(a,name.endswith('W')).lower(),0) if a else self.base)
            elif name in ['LoadLibraryA','LoadLibraryW','LoadLibraryExA','LoadLibraryExW']:
                module=self.text(a,name.endswith('W')).lower()
                if module not in self.modules: raise Unsupported('Module not modeled: '+module)
                self.ret(self.modules[module])
            elif name=='GetProcAddress':
                module=next((k for k,v in self.modules.items() if v==a),None)
                symbol=self.text(b) if b>65535 else '#'+str(b)
                target=self.exports.get((module,symbol))
                if not target: raise Unsupported('Export not modeled: '+str((module,symbol)))
                self.ret(target)
            elif name=='VirtualAlloc':
                if a: raise Unsupported('Fixed-address VirtualAlloc not modeled')
                self.ret(self.alloc(b))
            elif name=='VirtualProtect':
                self.uc.mem_write(d,struct.pack('<I',0x40)); self.ret(1)
            else: raise Unsupported('API not modeled: '+name)
        except (Unsupported,u.UcError) as error:
            self.api_failed=True; self.stop(str(error))
    def syscall(self,uc,data):
        self.in_syscall=True
        try: self.api(uc,self.reg('rip'),2,None)
        finally: self.in_syscall=False
    def image(self):
        image=self.uc.mem_read(self.base,self.size)
        for s in self.pe.sections:
            offset=s.get_file_offset(); size=align(s.Misc_VirtualSize)
            struct.pack_into('<I',image,offset+16,size)
            struct.pack_into('<I',image,offset+20,s.VirtualAddress)
        return image
    def dump(self,path):
        atomic_snapshot(path,self.image())
    def resources_ready(self,records):
        for r in records:
            start=r['data_rva']; size=r['size']
            if start<0 or start>self.size or size>self.size-start: return False
            if self.written.find(b'\0',start,start+size)!=-1: return False
        return True
    def scan_memory(self):
        image=self.image()
        candidates=self.scanner.scan(image,self.scan_hint)
        if len(candidates)!=1: self.scan_hint=None; return False
        table=candidates[0]; self.scan_hint=(table['table_rva'],table['key'])
        if not self.resources_ready(table['resources']): return False
        # A second table can appear as unpacking proceeds. Never let a hint hide it.
        candidates=self.scanner.scan(image)
        if len(candidates)!=1 or not self.resources_ready(candidates[0]['resources']): return False
        self.ready=True
        self.stop('Resource table and payloads available')
        return True
    def checkpoint(self,path):
        if getattr(self,'nonresumable_reason',None):
            raise Unsupported('Checkpoint refused: '+self.nonresumable_reason)
        # JSON register state and guest memory only; no executable pickle data.
        names=['rax','rbx','rcx','rdx','rsi','rdi','rbp','rsp','r8','r9','r10','r11','r12','r13','r14','r15','rip','eflags','gs_base','fs_base','mxcsr']+[f'xmm{i}' for i in range(16)]
        ctx=self.last_api_context if self.api_failed else self.uc.context_save()
        regs={name:ctx.reg_read(getattr(x,'UC_X86_REG_'+name.upper())) for name in names}
        state={key:getattr(self,key) for key in ['heap','allocations','next_handle','handles','thread_hidden','function_tables','events']}
        state['registers']=regs; state['regions']=list(self.uc.mem_regions())
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=1) as z:
            z.writestr('state.json',json.dumps(state))
            z.writestr('written.bin',self.written)
            for begin,end,perm in state['regions']: z.writestr(hex(begin),self.uc.mem_read(begin,end-begin+1))
    def restore(self,path):
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
            state=json.loads(z.read('state.json'))
            bitmap=z.read('written.bin')
            if len(bitmap)!=len(self.written): raise Unsupported('Checkpoint coverage size mismatch')
            regions=state.pop('regions')
            image=getattr(self,'host_image',None)
            # Reject a partial overlap before changing the existing mappings.
            # Protection changes can split the image into several valid regions.
            if image:
                image_regions=[]
                for begin,end,perm in regions:
                    if image.offset(begin,end-begin+1) is not None: image_regions.append((begin,end+1))
                cursor=image.base
                for begin,end in sorted(image_regions):
                    if begin!=cursor: raise Unsupported('Checkpoint image mappings overlap or are incomplete')
                    cursor=end
                if cursor!=image.base+image.size: raise Unsupported('Checkpoint image mappings are incomplete')
            self.written[:]=bitmap
            for begin,end,perm in list(self.uc.mem_regions()): self.uc.mem_unmap(begin,end-begin+1)
            for begin,end,perm in regions:
                size=end-begin+1
                if image and image.offset(begin,size) is not None: image.map_region(begin,size,perm)
                else: self.uc.mem_map(begin,size,perm)
                self.uc.mem_write(begin,z.read(hex(begin)))
            for name,value in state.pop('registers').items(): self.uc.reg_write(getattr(x,'UC_X86_REG_'+name.upper()),value)
            state['handles']={int(k):v for k,v in state['handles'].items()}
            for k,v in state.items(): setattr(self,k,v)
    def run(self,limit,seconds,snapshot,scheduler='stock'):
        if getattr(self,'nonresumable_reason',None):
            raise Unsupported('Cannot resume emulation: '+self.nonresumable_reason)
        print('Starting Unicorn emulation...',flush=True)
        start=time.monotonic(); batch=1000000; next_scan=100000000
        budget=BlockBudget(self.uc,limit,image=getattr(self,'host_image',None)) if scheduler=='block' else None
        budget_stats=None
        try:
            while self.steps<limit and not self.reason:
                remaining=seconds-(time.monotonic()-start)
                if remaining<=0: self.stop('Wall-clock budget exceeded'); break
                tick=time.monotonic()
                if budget:
                    budget.begin(batch,max(1,min(0xffffffff,int(remaining*1000))))
                    self.uc.emu_start(self.reg('rip'),0,timeout=max(1,int(remaining*1000000)),count=0)
                    self.steps=budget.get('total')
                    reason=budget.get('reason')
                    if not self.reason and reason>=2:
                        self.stop({2:'Instruction budget exceeded',3:'Wall-clock budget exceeded'}.get(reason,
                            f'Native block scheduler failed (reason {reason})'))
                else:
                    charged=min(batch,limit-self.steps)
                    self.uc.emu_start(self.reg('rip'),0,timeout=max(1,min(5_000_000,int(remaining*1000000))),count=charged)
                    self.steps+=charged
                self.metrics['emulation_seconds']+=time.monotonic()-tick
                # A timeout can interrupt native translation. Never resume that state.
                if self.uc.query(u.UC_QUERY_TIMEOUT):
                    self.stop('Unicorn execution timeout; state will not be resumed')
                    break
                if self.reason: break
                scan_due=self.steps>=next_scan
                if scan_due:
                    next_scan=self.steps+100000000
                    tick=time.monotonic(); self.metrics['scans']+=1
                    if self.scanner:
                        self.scan_memory()
                        scan=None
                    else:
                        self.dump(snapshot)
                        scan=scan_run(snapshot,'--list')
                    if scan is not None and scan.returncode==0:
                        with work_directory(prefix='resource-candidate-') as tmp:
                            out=Path(tmp)/'resources'
                            extract=scan_run(snapshot,'-o',out)
                            if extract.returncode==0:
                                metadata=json.loads((out/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
                                ready=all(all(self.written[int(r['data_rva'],16):int(r['data_rva'],16)+r['size']]) for r in metadata['resources'])
                                if ready:
                                    self.ready=True
                                    self.stop('Resource table and payloads available: '+scan.stdout.strip())
                    self.metrics['scan_seconds']+=time.monotonic()-tick
                if scan_due or self.reason:
                    print(f'budget={self.steps} rip={self.reg("rip"):#x} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
            if not self.reason: self.reason='Instruction budget exceeded'
        except u.UcError as error:
            if not self.reason: self.reason=str(error)
        finally:
            # Also check raised emu_start paths: these skip the normal query.
            # Timed-out state remains diagnostic only, including across runs.
            if self.uc.query(u.UC_QUERY_TIMEOUT):
                self.nonresumable_reason='Unicorn execution timeout; state will not be resumed'
                self.ready=False
                if not self.reason: self.reason=self.nonresumable_reason
                elif self.nonresumable_reason not in self.reason:
                    self.reason+='; '+self.nonresumable_reason
            if budget:
                budget_stats=budget.stats()
                budget.close()
        tick=time.monotonic(); self.dump(snapshot); self.metrics['snapshot_seconds']+=time.monotonic()-tick
        rip=self.reg('rip')
        try: disasm=[f'{i.address:#x}: {i.mnemonic} {i.op_str}' for i in self.md.disasm(bytes(self.uc.mem_read(rip,32)),rip)]
        except u.UcError: disasm=[]
        return {'stop':self.reason,'rip':hex(rip),'calls':dict(self.calls),'events':self.events[-50:],'recent_blocks':[hex(a) for a in self.blocks],'disassembly':disasm,'elapsed':time.monotonic()-start,'metrics':self.metrics,'native_coverage':self.native_coverage.stats() if self.native_coverage else None,'scheduler':scheduler,'block_budget':budget_stats,'resumable':not getattr(self,'nonresumable_reason',None),'nonresumable_reason':getattr(self,'nonresumable_reason',None)}

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('input',type=Path); p.add_argument('--snapshot',type=Path,help='Keep a diagnostic memory image and report')
    p.add_argument('-o','--output',type=Path,help='New output directory; default: INPUT.resources')
    p.add_argument('--instructions',type=int,default=5000000000,help='Positive instruction budget')
    p.add_argument('--seconds',type=int,default=240)
    p.add_argument('--resume',type=Path,help='Development checkpoint created by this tool')
    p.add_argument('--checkpoint',action='store_true',help='Save development state beside --snapshot')
    p.add_argument('--reference',action='store_true',help='Use the Python coverage and file-based scanner for comparison')
    p.add_argument('--scheduler',choices=['stock','block'],help='Instruction budget scheduler (default: native blocks when available)')
    args=p.parse_args()
    if not 0<args.instructions<2**64 or args.seconds<=0: p.error('Budgets must be positive; instruction budget must fit uint64')
    if args.checkpoint and not args.snapshot: p.error('--checkpoint requires --snapshot')
    output=(args.output or Path(str(args.input)+'.resources')).resolve()
    if output.exists() or not output.parent.is_dir(): p.error('Output must be a new directory with an existing parent')
    if args.snapshot:
        for path in [args.snapshot,args.snapshot.with_suffix('.json'),args.snapshot.with_suffix('.checkpoint.zip')]:
            if path.exists(): p.error('Diagnostic path already exists: '+str(path))
    emu=None
    try:
        before=hashlib.sha256(args.input.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        with work_directory(prefix='vmp-emulation-') as tmp:
            # Only the reference path uses periodic disk scans. Keep its working
            # image private and publish diagnostics after emulation ends.
            snapshot=Path(tmp)/'memory.exe'
            emu=Emulator(args.input,reference=args.reference)
            if args.resume: emu.restore(args.resume)
            scheduler=args.scheduler or ('block' if not args.reference and library_path('block_budget.dll').exists() else 'stock')
            result=emu.run(args.instructions,args.seconds,snapshot,scheduler=scheduler)
            result['input_sha256']=before
            result['input_unchanged']=hashlib.sha256(args.input.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==before
            if not result['input_unchanged']: raise Unsupported('Input changed during extraction')
            if args.checkpoint:
                if result['resumable']:
                    emu.checkpoint(args.snapshot.with_suffix('.checkpoint.zip'))
                else:
                    result['checkpoint_skipped']=result['nonresumable_reason']
                    print('Checkpoint skipped: '+result['checkpoint_skipped'],file=sys.stderr)
            if args.snapshot:
                atomic_snapshot(args.snapshot,snapshot.read_bytes())
                args.snapshot.with_suffix('.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
            if not getattr(emu,'ready',False):
                print('Extraction stopped: '+result['stop'],file=sys.stderr)
                return 1
            extracted=scan_run(snapshot,'-o',output)
            if extracted.returncode: raise Unsupported(extracted.stderr)
            manifest=output/'manifest.json'
            data=json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            data.update(input=args.input.name,input_sha256=before,method='unicorn-emulation',emulation_seconds=result['elapsed'],
                        payload_readiness='file-backed bytes or observed writes to initially empty image sections')
            manifest.write_text(json.dumps(data,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            print(f'Extracted {len(data["resources"])} resources in {result["elapsed"]:.1f}s.\nOutput: {output}')
            return 0
    except (RuntimeError,u.UcError,pefile.PEFormatError,OSError,ValueError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        print('error: '+str(error),file=sys.stderr); return 1
    finally:
        if emu and emu.native_coverage: emu.native_coverage.close()

if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())
```

`standalone/main.cpp`:

```cpp
#define NOMINMAX
#include <windows.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <set>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace fs = std::filesystem;
using Bytes = std::vector<uint8_t>;
constexpr uint32_t MAX_ENTRIES = 4096, MAX_RESOURCES = 16384;
constexpr uint64_t MAX_FILE = 1024ull*1024*1024, MAX_TOTAL = 512ull*1024*1024;
struct Invalid : std::runtime_error { using runtime_error::runtime_error; };
struct ScanTimedOut : std::runtime_error { ScanTimedOut():std::runtime_error("Resource scan exceeded time limit") {} };
struct ScanDeadline {
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point expires;
    mutable uint32_t checks=0;
    explicit ScanDeadline(uint32_t milliseconds):expires(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()+std::chrono::milliseconds(milliseconds)) {}
    void check(bool immediate=false) const {
        // Amortize clock reads while still checking both candidate loops and trees.
        if((immediate || (++checks&1023u)==0) && std::chrono::steady_clock::now()>=expires) throw ScanTimedOut();
    }
};
uint32_t rol(uint32_t x, unsigned n) { n &= 31; return (x << n) | (x >> ((32-n)&31)); }
std::string utf8(const std::wstring& s) {
    if(s.empty()) return {};
    int n=WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8,WC_ERR_INVALID_CHARS,s.data(),int(s.size()),nullptr,0,nullptr,nullptr);
    if(!n) throw Invalid("Invalid UTF-16 resource name");
    std::string r(n,'\0'); WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8,WC_ERR_INVALID_CHARS,s.data(),int(s.size()),r.data(),n,nullptr,nullptr); return r;
}
std::string hex(uint64_t n) { std::ostringstream s; s<<"0x"<<std::hex<<n; return s.str(); }
std::string quote(const std::string& s) {
    std::ostringstream o; o<<'"'; const char* h="0123456789abcdef";
    for(unsigned char c:s) { if(c=='"'||c=='\\') o<<'\\'<<c; else if(c<32) o<<"\\u00"<<h[c>>4]<<h[c&15]; else o<<c; } o<<'"'; return o.str();
}
struct Section { uint32_t va, raw, size; };
struct PE {
    Bytes b; std::vector<Section> sections; uint32_t headers=0; bool x64=false;
    void bounds(uint64_t p,uint64_t n) const { if(p>b.size()||n>b.size()-p) throw Invalid("Truncated PE or out-of-file range"); }
    uint16_t u16(uint64_t p) const { bounds(p,2); return b[size_t(p)] | uint16_t(b[size_t(p)+1])<<8; }
    uint32_t u32(uint64_t p) const { bounds(p,4); uint32_t v=0; for(int i=0;i<4;i++) v|=uint32_t(b[size_t(p)+i])<<(8*i); return v; }
    explicit PE(const fs::path& p) {
        auto len=fs::file_size(p); if(len<64||len>MAX_FILE) throw Invalid("Input size must be 64 bytes to 1 GiB");
        b.resize(size_t(len)); std::ifstream f(p,std::ios::binary); if(!f.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(b.data()),std::streamsize(len))) throw Invalid("Cannot read input file");
        parse();
    }
    explicit PE(Bytes data):b(std::move(data)) { parse(); }
    void parse() {
        if(b.size()<64||b.size()>MAX_FILE) throw Invalid("Input size must be 64 bytes to 1 GiB");
        if(u16(0)!=0x5a4d) throw Invalid("Not a DOS/PE image");
        uint64_t nt=u32(0x3c); if(u32(nt)!=0x4550) throw Invalid("Invalid PE signature");
        unsigned count=u16(nt+6), optsize=u16(nt+20); uint64_t opt=nt+24;
        if(!count||count>96||optsize<64) throw Invalid("Invalid section count or optional header");
        bounds(opt,optsize); auto magic=u16(opt); if(magic!=0x10b&&magic!=0x20b) throw Invalid("Unsupported PE optional header"); x64=magic==0x20b;
        headers=u32(opt+60); bounds(0,headers);
        uint64_t table=opt+optsize; bounds(table,uint64_t(count)*40);
        for(unsigned i=0;i<count;i++) {
            uint64_t q=table+i*40; Section s{u32(q+12),u32(q+20),u32(q+16)};
            if(!s.size) continue; bounds(s.raw,s.size);
            if(s.va<headers||s.raw<headers||uint64_t(s.va)+s.size>0x100000000ull) throw Invalid("Invalid section range");
            for(auto t:sections) if((uint64_t(s.va)<uint64_t(t.va)+t.size&&uint64_t(t.va)<uint64_t(s.va)+s.size) || (uint64_t(s.raw)<uint64_t(t.raw)+t.size&&uint64_t(t.raw)<uint64_t(s.raw)+s.size)) throw Invalid("Overlapping PE sections");
            sections.push_back(s);
        }
    }
    size_t map(uint64_t rva,uint64_t n) const {
        if(rva<headers&&n<=uint64_t(headers)-rva) { bounds(rva,n); return size_t(rva); }
        for(auto s:sections) if(rva>=s.va&&rva- s.va<s.size&&n<=uint64_t(s.size)-(rva-s.va)) return size_t(s.raw+(rva-s.va));
        throw Invalid("RVA is not fully backed by file data");
    }
};
struct ID { bool named=false; uint32_t number=0; std::string name;
    std::string json() const { return named?quote(name):std::to_string(number); }
};
struct Resource { std::array<ID,3> ids; uint32_t rva,size,codepage; size_t fileOffset; };
struct Table { uint32_t rva,key; std::vector<Resource> resources; uint64_t total=0; };
class Parser {
    const PE& pe; Table result; uint64_t tableEnd; std::set<uint32_t> dirs; std::set<std::string> paths;
    const ScanDeadline* deadline;
    uint32_t word(uint64_t offset) const {
        if(offset>tableEnd-result.rva || 4>tableEnd-result.rva-offset) throw Invalid("Table field outside its section");
        return pe.u32(pe.map(uint64_t(result.rva)+offset,4))^result.key;
    }
    ID id(uint32_t v) const {
        if(!(v&0x80000000)) { if(v>65535) throw Invalid("Resource ID out of range"); return {false,v,{}}; }
        uint64_t off=v&0x7fffffff; std::wstring s;
        for(unsigned j=0;j<4096;j++) {
            if(deadline) deadline->check();
            uint64_t rva=uint64_t(result.rva)+off+j*2;
            if(rva>=tableEnd||2>tableEnd-rva) throw Invalid("Name outside table section");
            uint16_t c=pe.u16(pe.map(rva,2))^uint16_t(rol(result.key,j)+j);
            if(!c) { if(s.empty()) throw Invalid("Empty resource name"); return {true,0,utf8(s)}; }
            s.push_back(wchar_t(c));
        }
        throw Invalid("Resource name exceeds 4095 UTF-16 units");
    }
    void walk(uint32_t offset,unsigned depth,std::array<ID,3> ids) {
        if(deadline) deadline->check();
        if(depth>2||!dirs.insert(offset).second||dirs.size()>MAX_RESOURCES) throw Invalid("Cyclic, shared or too deep resource directory");
        uint32_t named=word(offset), numeric=word(uint64_t(offset)+4);
        if(named>MAX_ENTRIES||numeric>MAX_ENTRIES||uint64_t(named)+numeric==0||uint64_t(named)+numeric>MAX_ENTRIES) throw Invalid("Invalid directory counts");
        std::set<std::string> identifiers;
        for(uint32_t i=0;i<named+numeric;i++) {
            if(deadline) deadline->check();
            uint64_t p=uint64_t(offset)+8+uint64_t(i)*8; uint32_t ident=word(p), target=word(p+4);
            if(bool(ident&0x80000000)!=(i<named)) throw Invalid("Named/integer entry ordering mismatch");
            ids[depth]=id(ident); if(!identifiers.insert(ids[depth].json()).second) throw Invalid("Duplicate identifier");
            if(depth<2) {
                if(!(target&0x80000000)) throw Invalid("Expected child directory");
                walk(target&0x7fffffff,depth+1,ids);
            } else {
                if((target&0x80000000)||ids[2].named) throw Invalid("Invalid language leaf");
                uint32_t rva=word(target), size=word(uint64_t(target)+4), cp=word(uint64_t(target)+8), reserved=word(uint64_t(target)+12);
                if(reserved||cp>65535||size>256u*1024*1024||result.resources.size()>=MAX_RESOURCES||result.total+size>MAX_TOTAL) throw Invalid("Invalid or oversized resource leaf");
                size_t fileOffset=pe.map(rva,size);
                std::string path=ids[0].json()+"/"+ids[1].json()+"/"+ids[2].json();
                if(!paths.insert(path).second) throw Invalid("Duplicate resource path");
                result.resources.push_back({ids,rva,size,cp,fileOffset}); result.total+=size;
            }
        }
    }
public:
    Parser(const PE& p,uint32_t rva,uint32_t key,const ScanDeadline* limit=nullptr):pe(p),result{rva,key,{},0},tableEnd(0),deadline(limit) {
        for(auto s:p.sections) if(rva>=s.va&&uint64_t(rva)-s.va<s.size) tableEnd=uint64_t(s.va)+s.size;
        if(!tableEnd) throw Invalid("Table is not in a file-backed section");
    }
    Table parse() { if(deadline) deadline->check(true); walk(0,0,{}); if(result.resources.empty()) throw Invalid("Empty resource tree"); return result; }
};

std::vector<Table> detect(const PE& pe,const ScanDeadline* deadline=nullptr) {
    if(deadline) deadline->check(true);
    std::vector<Table> found; uint64_t attempts=0;
    for(auto s:pe.sections) {
        if(s.size<24) continue;
        for(uint64_t pos=s.raw;pos+24<=uint64_t(s.raw)+s.size;pos++) {
            if(deadline) deadline->check();
            uint32_t a=pe.u32(pos), b=pe.u32(pos+4), diff=a^b;
            if(diff>8191) continue;
            // XOR cancels the key. Root counts bound the unknown low key bits.
            uint32_t ident=pe.u32(pos+8)^a, target=pe.u32(pos+12)^a;
            if(!(target&0x80000000)) continue;
            uint32_t off=target&0x7fffffff;
            uint64_t available=uint64_t(s.raw)+s.size-pos;
            if((off&~8191u)>=available) continue;
            uint32_t rva=s.va+uint32_t(pos-s.raw);
            for(uint32_t named=0;named<=MAX_ENTRIES;named++) {
                if(deadline) deadline->check();
                uint32_t numeric=diff^named;
                if(numeric>MAX_ENTRIES||named+numeric==0||named+numeric>MAX_ENTRIES) continue;
                if(bool(ident&0x80000000)!=(named!=0)) continue;
                if(!named && (ident^named)>65535) continue;
                uint32_t child=off^named;
                if(child<8+8*(named+numeric)||uint64_t(child)+8>available) continue;
                uint32_t key=a^named;
                uint32_t ca=pe.u32(pos+child)^key, cb=pe.u32(pos+child+4)^key;
                if(ca>MAX_ENTRIES||cb>MAX_ENTRIES||uint64_t(ca)+cb==0||uint64_t(ca)+cb>MAX_ENTRIES) continue;
                if(++attempts>1000000) throw Invalid("Too many candidate trees; supply --table-rva and --key");
                try {
                    auto table=Parser(pe,rva,key,deadline).parse(); found.push_back(std::move(table));
                    if(found.size()>64) throw std::runtime_error("Too many valid tables; use explicit --table-rva and --key");
                } catch(const Invalid&) {}
            }
        }
    }
    if(deadline) deadline->check(true);
    return found;
}

void writeFile(const fs::path& path,const char* bytes,size_t n) {
    std::ofstream f(path,std::ios::binary); if(!f) throw std::runtime_error("Cannot create output file");
    f.write(bytes,std::streamsize(n)); f.close(); if(!f) throw std::runtime_error("Output write failed");
}
void extract(const PE& pe,const Table& t,const fs::path& input,const fs::path& output) {
    if(fs::exists(output)) throw Invalid("Output directory already exists; choose a new --output directory");
    if(!fs::is_directory(output.parent_path())) throw Invalid("Output parent directory does not exist");
    fs::path staging=output; staging+=L".partial-"+std::to_wstring(GetCurrentProcessId());
    if(!fs::create_directory(staging)) throw Invalid("Temporary output directory already exists");
    try {
        std::ostringstream manifest;
        manifest<<"{\n  \"format\": \"vmprotect-resource-extractor-1\",\n  \"input\": "<<quote(utf8(input.filename().wstring()))
          <<",\n  \"architecture\": "<<quote(pe.x64?"x64":"x32")<<",\n  \"table_rva\": "<<quote(hex(t.rva))<<",\n  \"key\": "<<quote(hex(t.key))
          <<",\n  \"validation\": \"structural; plaintext is not authenticated\",\n  \"resources\": [\n";
        for(size_t index=0;index<t.resources.size();index++) {
            const auto& r=t.resources[index]; Bytes plain(r.size);
            for(uint32_t i=0;i<r.size;i++) plain[i]=pe.b[r.fileOffset+i]^uint8_t(rol(t.key,i)+i);
            char filename[64]; snprintf(filename,sizeof(filename),"resource-%04zu.bin",index+1);
            writeFile(staging/filename,reinterpret_cast<const char*>(plain.data()),plain.size());
            manifest<<"    {\"type\": "<<r.ids[0].json()<<", \"name\": "<<r.ids[1].json()<<", \"language\": "<<r.ids[2].json()<<", \"codepage\": "<<r.codepage<<", \"data_rva\": "<<quote(hex(r.rva))<<", \"size\": "<<r.size<<", \"file\": "<<quote(filename)<<"}"<<(index+1<t.resources.size()?",":"")<<"\n";
        }
        manifest<<"  ]\n}\n"; auto m=manifest.str(); writeFile(staging/L"manifest.json",m.data(),m.size());
        fs::rename(staging,output);
    } catch(...) { std::error_code ec; fs::remove_all(staging,ec); throw; }
}
uint32_t number(const std::wstring& s) {
    size_t end=0; if(s.empty()||s[0]==L'-') throw Invalid("Invalid numeric argument");
    auto n=std::stoull(s,&end,0); if(end!=s.size()||n>0xffffffffull) throw Invalid("Numeric argument outside DWORD range"); return uint32_t(n);
}
void help() {
    std::cout<<"VMProtect Resource Extractor - standalone\n\n"
      "Usage: vmp-resource-extractor.exe input.exe [--output directory] [--list]\n"
      "       vmp-resource-extractor.exe input.dll --table-rva 0x... --key 0x...\n\n"
      "Reads the PE file without executing it. Extracts the private resource tree\n"
      "to numbered binary files plus manifest.json (type, name, language, codepage).\n"
      "The original file is never patched. An existing output folder is not replaced.\n"
      "--list lists structurally valid candidate tables without extracting.\n"
      "Default output: <input filename>.resources next to the input.\n"
      "Use 0x prefixes for hexadecimal arguments.\n";
}
#ifndef RESOURCE_SCANNER_LIBRARY
int wmain(int argc,wchar_t** argv) {
    try {
        if(argc<2) { help(); return 0; }
        fs::path input,output; bool list=false,hasRva=false,hasKey=false; uint32_t rva=0,key=0;
        for(int i=1;i<argc;i++) {
            std::wstring a=argv[i];
            auto next=[&](){ if(i+1>=argc) throw Invalid("Missing option value"); return std::wstring(argv[++i]); };
            if(a==L"--help"||a==L"-h") {help();return 0;}
            else if(a==L"--output"||a==L"-o") output=next();
            else if(a==L"--list") list=true;
            else if(a==L"--table-rva") {rva=number(next());hasRva=true;}
            else if(a==L"--key") {key=number(next());hasKey=true;}
            else if(!a.empty()&&a[0]==L'-') throw Invalid("Unknown option");
            else if(input.empty()) input=a; else throw Invalid("Specify one input file");
        }
        if(input.empty()||hasRva!=hasKey) throw Invalid("Specify an input and supply both --table-rva and --key, or neither");
        input=fs::absolute(input); PE pe(input);
        std::vector<Table> tables;
        if(hasRva) tables.push_back(Parser(pe,rva,key).parse()); else tables=detect(pe);
        if(tables.empty()) throw Invalid("No supported private resource tree found. The file may be unpacked, unsupported, damaged or require runtime unpacking");
        for(const auto& t:tables) std::cout<<"table="<<hex(t.rva)<<" key="<<hex(t.key)<<" resources="<<t.resources.size()<<" bytes="<<t.total<<"\n";
        if(list) return 0;
        if(tables.size()!=1) throw Invalid("Multiple valid tables found; select one using --table-rva and --key");
        if(output.empty()) { output=input; output+=L".resources"; } else output=fs::absolute(output);
        extract(pe,tables[0],input,output);
        std::cout<<"Extracted "<<tables[0].resources.size()<<" resources.\nOutput: "<<utf8(output.wstring())<<"\n";
        return 0;
    } catch(const std::exception& e) {std::cerr<<"error: "<<e.what()<<"\n";return 1;}
}
#endif
```
