`standalone/native_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
// The CLI and DLL deliberately share the parser and all structural limits.
#define RESOURCE_SCANNER_LIBRARY
#include "main.cpp"
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>

// ABI v1, cdecl. Input is a complete file-layout PE buffer, not a host pointer
// to guest memory. Callers must provide a readable buffer of input_size bytes.
// Every non-null response is malloc-owned and must be released with
// resource_scanner_free, including error responses. Size excludes trailing NUL.
// Status: 0 success; 1 no candidate; 2 invalid input; 3 allocation failure;
//         4 internal error; 5 time limit. A valid hint selects that revalidated table;
// an invalid hint falls back to complete detection.
// Scans have a 20-second limit. The additive scan_timed entry point accepts a
// shorter timeout in milliseconds (0 fails immediately), capped at 20 seconds.
namespace {
int reply(int status,const std::string& json,char** output,size_t* output_size) {
    char* result=static_cast<char*>(std::malloc(json.size()+1));
    if(!result) return 3;
    std::memcpy(result,json.data(),json.size()); result[json.size()]='\0';
    *output=result; *output_size=json.size(); return status;
}
std::string metadata(const std::vector<Table>& tables,bool hinted,const ScanDeadline& deadline) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out<<"{\"abi\":1,\"hint_used\":"<<(hinted?"true":"false")<<",\"candidates\":[";
    for(size_t i=0;i<tables.size();i++) {
        const auto& table=tables[i];
        if(i) out<<',';
        out<<"{\"table_rva\":"<<table.rva<<",\"key\":"<<table.key
           <<",\"total\":"<<table.total<<",\"resources\":[";
        for(size_t j=0;j<table.resources.size();j++) {
            deadline.check();
            const auto& resource=table.resources[j];
            if(j) out<<',';
            out<<"{\"type\":"<<resource.ids[0].json()<<",\"name\":"<<resource.ids[1].json()
               <<",\"language\":"<<resource.ids[2].json()<<",\"data_rva\":"<<resource.rva
               <<",\"size\":"<<resource.size<<",\"codepage\":"<<resource.codepage<<'}';
        }
        out<<"]}";
    }
    out<<"]}"; return out.str();
}
}

extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) unsigned __cdecl resource_scanner_abi() noexcept { return 1; }

extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) void __cdecl resource_scanner_free(void* response) noexcept {
    std::free(response);
}

extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) int __cdecl resource_scanner_scan_timed(
    const uint8_t* input,size_t input_size,int use_hint,uint32_t table_rva,
    uint32_t key,uint32_t timeout_ms,char** output,size_t* output_size) noexcept {
    if(!output||!output_size) return 2;
    *output=nullptr; *output_size=0;
    try {
        if(!input||input_size<64||input_size>MAX_FILE) throw Invalid("Input size must be 64 bytes to 1 GiB");
        ScanDeadline deadline(std::min(timeout_ms,20000u));
        deadline.check(true);
        PE pe(Bytes(input,input+input_size));
        deadline.check(true);
        std::vector<Table> tables;
        bool hinted=false;
        if(use_hint) {
            try { tables.push_back(Parser(pe,table_rva,key,&deadline).parse()); hinted=true; }
            catch(const Invalid&) { /* Stale hint: discover again with full validation. */ }
        }
        if(tables.empty()) tables=detect(pe,&deadline);
        const int status=tables.empty()?1:0;
        auto json=metadata(tables,hinted,deadline);
        deadline.check(true);
        return reply(status,json,output,output_size);
    } catch(const std::bad_alloc&) {
        return 3;
    } catch(const ScanTimedOut& error) {
        try { return reply(5,"{\"abi\":1,\"error\":"+quote(error.what())+"}",output,output_size); }
        catch(...) { return 3; }
    } catch(const Invalid& error) {
        try { return reply(2,"{\"abi\":1,\"error\":"+quote(error.what())+"}",output,output_size); }
        catch(...) { return 3; }
    } catch(const std::exception& error) {
        try { return reply(4,"{\"abi\":1,\"error\":"+quote(error.what())+"}",output,output_size); }
        catch(...) { return 3; }
    } catch(...) {
        return 4;
    }
}

extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) int __cdecl resource_scanner_scan(
    const uint8_t* input,size_t input_size,int use_hint,uint32_t table_rva,
    uint32_t key,char** output,size_t* output_size) noexcept {
    return resource_scanner_scan_timed(input,input_size,use_hint,table_rva,key,20000,output,output_size);
}
```

`standalone/native_support.py`:

```py
"""Checked ctypes adapters for the optional local accelerators."""
import ctypes as c
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import capstone
import unicorn as u
from unicorn.unicorn_py3.unicorn import uclib

def library_path(name):
    return (Path(sys._MEIPASS) if getattr(sys,'frozen',False) else Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'dist')/name

class HostImage:
    """Fixed backing for the main image. Mutate guest bytes through uc.mem_write."""
    def __init__(self,uc,base,size):
        if not 0 <= base < 2**64 or not 0 < size <= min(2**64-base,sys.maxsize-4095):
            raise ValueError('Invalid host image range')
        if base%4096 or size%4096: raise ValueError('Host image must be page aligned')
        self.uc=uc
        self.base=base
        self.size=size
        self._buffer=(c.c_uint8*(size+4095))()
        self._pointer=(c.addressof(self._buffer)+4095)&~4095
        # Unicorn does not retain the Python allocation passed to mem_map_ptr.
        # Pin the raw buffer on the engine too, without an engine reference cycle.
        if not hasattr(uc,'_vmp_image_backings'): uc._vmp_image_backings=[]
        uc._vmp_image_backings.append(self._buffer)
    @property
    def pointer(self): return self._pointer
    def offset(self,begin,size):
        if not 0 <= begin < 2**64 or not 0 < size <= 2**64-begin:
            raise ValueError('Invalid guest mapping range')
        end=begin+size
        if self.base <= begin and end <= self.base+self.size: return begin-self.base
        if end <= self.base or begin >= self.base+self.size: return None
        raise ValueError('Guest mapping crosses the host image boundary')
    def map_region(self,begin,size,perms=u.UC_PROT_ALL):
        offset=self.offset(begin,size)
        if offset is None: raise ValueError('Mapping is outside the host image')
        self.uc.mem_map_ptr(begin,size,perms,self.pointer+offset)

class Coverage:
    """Pin the bitmap until close removes every native hook; never resize it."""
    def __init__(self,uc,base,written):
        if not written or not 0 <= base <= 0xffffffffffffffff or len(written)-1 > 0xffffffffffffffff-base:
            raise ValueError('Invalid native coverage image range')
        self.uc=uc
        self.base=base
        self.size=len(written)
        self.hooks=[]
        self.closed=False
        self.lib=c.CDLL(str(library_path('vmp-native-tracker.dll')))
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_abi_version.restype=c.c_uint
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_state_size.restype=c.c_size_t
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_callback.restype=c.c_void_p
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_init.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_size_t,c.c_uint64,c.c_uint64,c.c_void_p,c.c_size_t]
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_init.restype=c.c_int
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_stats.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_void_p,c.c_size_t]
        self.lib.vmp_tracker_stats.restype=c.c_int
        if self.lib.vmp_tracker_abi_version()!=1: raise RuntimeError('Unsupported coverage ABI')
        self.view=(c.c_uint8*len(written)).from_buffer(written)
        self.storage=c.create_string_buffer(self.lib.vmp_tracker_state_size())
        if not self.lib.vmp_tracker_init(self.storage,len(self.storage),base,len(written),self.view,len(written)):
            raise RuntimeError('Native coverage initialization failed')
    def attach(self,begin,end):
        if self.closed: raise RuntimeError('Native coverage is closed')
        if not self.base <= begin <= end < self.base+self.size:
            raise ValueError('Native coverage hook is outside the image')
        handle=c.c_size_t()
        error=uclib.uc_hook_add(self.uc._uch,c.byref(handle),u.UC_HOOK_MEM_WRITE,
            self.lib.vmp_tracker_callback(),c.cast(self.storage,c.c_void_p),c.c_uint64(begin),c.c_uint64(end))
        if error:
            failure=u.UcError(error)
            try: self.close()
            except Exception as cleanup_error:
                failure.add_note('Native hook cleanup failed: '+str(cleanup_error))
            raise failure
        self.hooks.append(handle.value)
    def close(self):
        # Keep failed handles and their backing objects alive so cleanup can be
        # retried. The caller must keep this adapter alive while hooks remain.
        remaining=[]; errors=[]
        for handle in self.hooks:
            error=uclib.uc_hook_del(self.uc._uch,handle)
            if error:
                remaining.append(handle); errors.append(error)
        self.hooks=remaining
        self.closed=not remaining
        if errors: raise u.UcError(errors[0])
    def stats(self):
        values=(c.c_uint64*5)()
        if not self.lib.vmp_tracker_stats(self.storage,values,5): raise RuntimeError('Native coverage stats failed')
        return dict(zip(['callbacks','writes','bytes','generation','rejected'],values))

class Scanner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.last_issue=None
        self.lib=c.CDLL(str(library_path('resource_scanner.dll')))
        self.lib.resource_scanner_scan.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_size_t,c.c_int,c.c_uint32,c.c_uint32,c.POINTER(c.c_void_p),c.POINTER(c.c_size_t)]
        self.lib.resource_scanner_scan.restype=c.c_int
        self.lib.resource_scanner_free.argtypes=[c.c_void_p]
        self.lib.resource_scanner_free.restype=None
        if self.lib.resource_scanner_abi()!=1: raise RuntimeError('Unsupported scanner ABI')
    def scan(self,image,hint=None):
        view=(c.c_uint8*len(image)).from_buffer(image)
        out=c.c_void_p(); size=c.c_size_t()
        try:
            status=self.lib.resource_scanner_scan(view,len(image),int(hint is not None),*(hint or (0,0)),c.byref(out),c.byref(size))
            result=json.loads(c.string_at(out,size.value).decode('utf-8')) if out else {}
            self.last_issue=result if status==2 else None
            # Unpacking can temporarily leave headers incomplete. Match the file
            # scanner's retry behavior, retaining the diagnostic for the report.
            if status==2: return []
            if status not in (0,1):
                label={3:'allocation failure',4:'internal error',5:'time limit'}.get(status,'unknown status')
                raise RuntimeError(f'Native scan failed ({status}: {label}): {result}')
            return result.get('candidates',[])
        finally:
            if out: self.lib.resource_scanner_free(out)

class BlockBudget:
    """Synchronous block boundaries; asynchronous timeout is terminal to callers."""
    def __init__(self,uc,total,image=None):
        if image is not None and image.uc is not uc:
            raise ValueError('Block budget image belongs to another Unicorn engine')
        self.uc=uc
        self.image=image
        self.lib=c.CDLL(str(library_path('block_budget.dll')))
        self.lib.bb_create.argtypes=[c.c_wchar_p,c.c_void_p,c.c_void_p,c.c_uint64]
        self.lib.bb_create.restype=c.c_void_p
        self.lib.bb_destroy.argtypes=[c.c_void_p]
        self.lib.bb_destroy.restype=None
        self.lib.bb_begin.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_uint64,c.c_uint32]
        self.lib.bb_begin.restype=c.c_int
        for name in ['reason','total','slice','blocks','decodes','hits']:
            method=getattr(self.lib,'bb_'+name)
            method.argtypes=[c.c_void_p]
            method.restype=c.c_uint32 if name=='reason' else c.c_uint64
        self.state=self.lib.bb_create(str(Path(capstone.__file__).parent/'lib/capstone.dll'),
            c.cast(uclib.uc_mem_read,c.c_void_p),c.cast(uclib.uc_emu_stop,c.c_void_p),total)
        if not self.state: raise RuntimeError('Native block scheduler initialization failed')
        if image is not None:
            try:
                self.lib.bb_set_image.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_uint64,c.c_uint64,c.c_void_p]
                self.lib.bb_set_image.restype=c.c_int
                if not self.lib.bb_set_image(self.state,image.base,image.size,image.pointer):
                    raise RuntimeError('Native block scheduler image binding failed')
            except Exception:
                self.lib.bb_destroy(self.state); self.state=None
                raise
        self.hook=c.c_size_t()
        error=uclib.uc_hook_add(uc._uch,c.byref(self.hook),u.UC_HOOK_BLOCK,
            c.cast(self.lib.bb_callback,c.c_void_p),self.state,c.c_uint64(1),c.c_uint64(0))
        if error:
            self.lib.bb_destroy(self.state); self.state=None
            raise u.UcError(error)
    def begin(self,limit,milliseconds):
        if not self.lib.bb_begin(self.state,limit,milliseconds):
            raise RuntimeError('Invalid native block budget')
    def get(self,name): return getattr(self.lib,'bb_'+name)(self.state)
    def stats(self): return {name:self.get(name) for name in ['total','blocks','decodes','hits']}
    def close(self):
        if self.state:
            error=uclib.uc_hook_del(self.uc._uch,self.hook)
            if error: raise u.UcError(error)
            self.lib.bb_destroy(self.state); self.state=None
```

`standalone/native_tracker.cpp`:

```cpp
// Allocation-free byte coverage tracking for Unicorn memory-write events.
// This DLL does not link Unicorn or modify guest registers/memory. The caller
// owns the state and coverage buffer and must keep both alive until hook removal.
#define NOMINMAX
#pragma warning(push)
#pragma warning(disable: 4505) // Unused static helpers in upstream platform.h.
#include <unicorn/unicorn.h>
#pragma warning(pop)
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <new>
#include <type_traits>

#define VMP_EXPORT extern "C" __declspec(dllexport)

namespace {
constexpr std::uint64_t magic = 0x564d505752495445ull;
struct Tracker {
    std::uint64_t signature;
    std::uint64_t base;
    std::uint64_t size;
    std::uint8_t* written;
    std::uint64_t callbacks;
    std::uint64_t accepted;
    std::uint64_t bytes;
    std::uint64_t generation;
    std::uint64_t rejected;
};

void saturating_add(std::uint64_t& value, std::uint64_t delta) noexcept {
    constexpr auto maximum = std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max();
    value = delta > maximum - value ? maximum : value + delta;
}

void write_hook(uc_engine*, uc_mem_type access, std::uint64_t address,
                int size, std::int64_t, void* opaque) noexcept {
    auto* tracker = static_cast<Tracker*>(opaque);
    if (!tracker || tracker->signature != magic) return;
    saturating_add(tracker->callbacks, 1);
    // Complete ranges only, matching the original Python tracker. Subtraction
    // avoids overflow at UINT64_MAX; never clamp a partial write into the image.
    if (access != UC_MEM_WRITE || size <= 0 || address < tracker->base) {
        saturating_add(tracker->rejected, 1);
        return;
    }
    const auto offset = address - tracker->base;
    const auto length = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(size);
    if (offset > tracker->size || length > tracker->size - offset) {
        saturating_add(tracker->rejected, 1);
        return;
    }
    std::memset(tracker->written + static_cast<std::size_t>(offset), 1,
                static_cast<std::size_t>(length));
    saturating_add(tracker->accepted, 1);
    saturating_add(tracker->bytes, length);
    // Generation records accepted writes, including rewrites of covered bytes.
    saturating_add(tracker->generation, 1);
}
static_assert(std::is_convertible_v<decltype(&write_hook), uc_cb_hookmem_t>);
}

VMP_EXPORT unsigned vmp_tracker_abi_version() noexcept { return 1; }
VMP_EXPORT std::size_t vmp_tracker_state_size() noexcept { return sizeof(Tracker); }
VMP_EXPORT void* vmp_tracker_callback() noexcept {
    return reinterpret_cast<void*>(&write_hook);
}

// Return 1 on success, 0 on invalid capacity/alignment/range. Initial coverage
// is retained: the caller can mark file-backed bytes before attaching the hook.
VMP_EXPORT int vmp_tracker_init(void* storage, std::size_t storage_size,
                               std::uint64_t base, std::uint64_t size,
                               std::uint8_t* written, std::size_t capacity) noexcept {
    if (!storage || storage_size < sizeof(Tracker) ||
        reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(storage) % alignof(Tracker) != 0 ||
        !written || size > capacity || size > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() ||
        (size && size - 1 > std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max() - base)) return 0;
    new (storage) Tracker{magic, base, size, written, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    return 1;
}

// values: callbacks, accepted writes, accepted bytes (rewrites included),
// generation, rejected writes. Read only when Unicorn is not running.
VMP_EXPORT int vmp_tracker_stats(const void* storage, std::uint64_t* values,
                                std::size_t count) noexcept {
    const auto* tracker = static_cast<const Tracker*>(storage);
    if (!tracker || tracker->signature != magic || !values || count < 5) return 0;
    values[0] = tracker->callbacks;
    values[1] = tracker->accepted;
    values[2] = tracker->bytes;
    values[3] = tracker->generation;
    values[4] = tracker->rejected;
    return 1;
}

```

`standalone/work_files.py`:

```py
"""Windows temporary work directories with inherited user-directory ACLs."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
import uuid

@contextmanager
def work_directory(prefix='vmp-',root=None):
    parent=Path(root or tempfile.gettempdir()).resolve()
    path=parent/(prefix+uuid.uuid4().hex)
    # Python 3.14's mode-0700 Windows ACL is incompatible with restricted tokens.
    # The user's TEMP directory supplies the access policy instead.
    path.mkdir(mode=0o777)
    try:
        yield str(path)
    finally:
        if path.parent.resolve()!=parent: raise RuntimeError('Unexpected temporary directory parent')
        shutil.rmtree(path)

```

`tests/benchmark_emulation.py`:

```py
"""Sequential packed-fixture benchmarks with independent resource verification.

Examples:
  py tests/benchmark_emulation.py --mode source --samples paths
  py tests/benchmark_emulation.py --mode source --reference
  py tests/benchmark_emulation.py --mode packaged

All generated files stay in a new build/optimization directory. Inputs and
original fixtures are only read; timings include verification-free process time.
"""
import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid

import unicorn
from verify_samples import SAMPLES as FIXTURE_ROOT, original

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SAMPLE_DIR=FIXTURE_ROOT/'originals/x64'
SAMPLES={
    'paths':('ResourcePaths-x64.vmp.exe','ResourcePaths-x64.exe'),
    'sample':('ResourceSample-x64.vmp.exe','ResourceSample-x64.exe'),
    'fixture':('pawdResourceFixture-x64.dll','ResourceFixture-x64.dll'),
}

def sha256(path):
    digest=hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''): digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def verify(sample,source,output,input_sha256):
    metadata=json.loads((output/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    expected={key:value for key,value in original(source).items() if key[0]!=24}
    actual={}; hashes={}
    for resource in metadata['resources']:
        key=(resource['type'],resource['name'],resource['language'])
        if key in actual: raise ValueError('Duplicate extracted resource: '+str(key))
        path=(output/resource['file']).resolve()
        if not path.is_relative_to(output.resolve()): raise ValueError('Resource escaped output directory')
        payload=path.read_bytes()
        actual[key]=(payload,resource['codepage'])
        hashes[resource['file']]=hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
    if actual!=expected: raise ValueError('Extracted resource bytes or metadata do not match original fixture')
    if metadata['method']!='unicorn-emulation': raise ValueError('Unexpected extraction method')
    if metadata['input_sha256']!=input_sha256: raise ValueError('Manifest input hash does not match sample')
    return {'matches_original':True,'resource_count':len(actual),
            'resource_bytes':sum(len(value[0]) for value in actual.values()),
            'emulation_seconds':metadata['emulation_seconds'],'resource_sha256':hashes}

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument('--mode',choices=['source','packaged'],default='source')
    parser.add_argument('--executable',type=Path,help='Packaged executable override')
    parser.add_argument('--reference',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--scheduler',choices=['stock','block'],default='stock')
    parser.add_argument('--samples',nargs='+',choices=list(SAMPLES),default=list(SAMPLES))
    parser.add_argument('--repeats',type=int,default=1)
    parser.add_argument('--seconds',type=int,default=600)
    parser.add_argument('--instructions',type=int,default=15000000000)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    if min(args.repeats,args.seconds,args.instructions)<=0: parser.error('Budgets and repeats must be positive')
    if args.executable and args.mode!='packaged': parser.error('--executable requires --mode packaged')
    executable=(args.executable or ROOT/'dist/vmp-resource-emulator.exe').resolve()
    target=ROOT/'standalone/emulate.py' if args.mode=='source' else executable
    if not target.is_file(): parser.error('Missing emulator: '+str(target))
    for name in args.samples:
        for filename in SAMPLES[name]:
            if not (SAMPLE_DIR/filename).is_file(): parser.error('Missing sample: '+filename)
    stamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')+'-'+uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    output_root=ROOT/'build/optimization'/stamp
    output_root.mkdir(parents=True)
    artifacts=[target]
    if args.mode=='source':
        artifacts+=list((ROOT/'standalone').glob('*.py'))+list((ROOT/'dist').glob('*.dll'))
        artifacts.append(ROOT/'dist/vmp-resource-extractor.exe')
    summary={'started_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'mode':args.mode,
             'reference':args.reference,'scheduler':args.scheduler,'python':sys.version,
             'unicorn':unicorn.__version__,'instructions':args.instructions,'seconds':args.seconds,
             'artifacts':{str(path):sha256(path) for path in dict.fromkeys(artifacts) if path.is_file()},'runs':[]}
    summary_path=output_root/'summary.json'
    failed=False
    for repeat in range(1,args.repeats+1):
        for name in args.samples:
            directory=output_root/f'{name}-{repeat}'
            directory.mkdir()
            sample,source=(SAMPLE_DIR/filename for filename in SAMPLES[name])
            before={str(path):sha256(path) for path in (sample,source)}
            output=directory/'resources'; snapshot=directory/'memory.exe'
            command=[sys.executable,str(target)] if args.mode=='source' else [str(target)]
            command += [str(sample),'-o',str(output),'--snapshot',str(snapshot),
                        '--instructions',str(args.instructions),'--seconds',str(args.seconds)]
            command += ['--scheduler',args.scheduler]
            if args.reference: command.append('--reference')
            run={'sample':sample.name,'repeat':repeat,'command':command,'input_sha256':before[str(sample)],
                 'original_sha256':before[str(source)],'directory':str(directory)}
            print(f'Running {name} #{repeat}: {args.mode}, {args.scheduler}, reference={args.reference}',flush=True)
            started=time.perf_counter()
            try:
                with (directory/'stdout.log').open('wb') as stdout,(directory/'stderr.log').open('wb') as stderr:
                    process=subprocess.run(command,cwd=ROOT,stdout=stdout,stderr=stderr,timeout=args.seconds+180)
                run['returncode']=process.returncode
                run['wall_seconds']=time.perf_counter()-started
                if process.returncode: raise RuntimeError('Emulator failed; see stderr.log and diagnostic report')
                run.update(verify(sample,source,output,before[str(sample)]))
            except (OSError,RuntimeError,ValueError,KeyError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
                run['wall_seconds']=time.perf_counter()-started
                run['error']=str(error); failed=True
            after={str(path):sha256(path) for path in (sample,source)}
            run['inputs_unchanged']=after==before
            if not run['inputs_unchanged']:
                run['error']='Sample or original fixture changed during benchmark'; failed=True
            diagnostic=snapshot.with_suffix('.json')
            if diagnostic.exists():
                run['diagnostic']=json.loads(diagnostic.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            summary['runs'].append(run)
            (directory/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(run,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            print(f'{name}: {run["wall_seconds"]:.3f}s; '+('FAIL '+run['error'] if 'error' in run else 'verified'),flush=True)
            if not run['inputs_unchanged']:
                print('Stopped because an input changed.',file=sys.stderr)
                print(summary_path)
                return 1
    print(summary_path)
    return int(failed)

if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

```

`tests/plugin_backend_harness.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "../plugin/backend.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <iterator>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

int wmain(int argc,wchar_t** argv) {
    try {
        if(argc<4) throw std::runtime_error("memory-image output base [mode] [value]");
        const std::filesystem::path input=argv[1], output=argv[2];
        const uint64_t base=std::stoull(argv[3],nullptr,0);
        const std::wstring mode=argc>4?argv[4]:L"normal";
        const uint64_t value=argc>5?std::stoull(argv[5],nullptr,0):0;
        std::ifstream file(input,std::ios::binary);
        std::vector<unsigned char> memory((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(file)),{});
        if(!file || memory.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("Cannot read test memory image");
        size_t reads=0, headerReads=0;
        vre::Module selected{base,memory.size(),L"memory-fixture.exe",input.wstring()};
        auto snapshot=vre::capture(selected,[&](uint64_t address,void* buffer,size_t size) {
            ++reads;
            if(address<base || address-base>memory.size() || size>memory.size()-(address-base)) return false;
            const uint64_t offset=address-base;
            if(mode==L"blocked" && offset<value+4096 && value<offset+size) return false;
            std::memcpy(buffer,memory.data()+offset,size);
            if(offset==0) {
                ++headerReads;
                if((headerReads>=5 && mode==L"changed") || (headerReads>=3 && mode==L"early-change"))
                    static_cast<unsigned char*>(buffer)[0]^=1;
            }
            return true;
        },[&] { return mode!=L"cancel" || reads<value; });
        const auto& list=vre::candidates(snapshot);
        std::cout<<"candidates="<<list.size()<<" reads="<<reads<<"\n";
        if(mode==L"list") return 0;
        if(list.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("No resource table found");
        if(mode==L"freeze") std::fill(memory.begin(),memory.end(),static_cast<unsigned char>(0));
        const size_t index=mode==L"select"?size_t(value):0;
        const auto result=vre::extract_snapshot(snapshot,index,output);
        std::cout<<"resources="<<result.resources<<" bytes="<<result.bytes<<"\n";
        return 0;
    } catch(const std::exception& error) {
        std::cerr<<"error: "<<error.what()<<"\n";
        return 1;
    }
}
```

`tests/test_block_budget.py`:

```py
"""Experimental native block scheduling: parity, bounds, SMC and synchronous exits."""
import ctypes as C
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import time
import capstone
import unicorn as U
from unicorn import x86_const as X
from unicorn.unicorn_py3.unicorn import uclib

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BASE=0x100000

class BlockBudget:
    def __init__(self,uc,total,read=None):
        self.uc=uc
        self.dll=C.CDLL(str(ROOT/'dist/block_budget.dll'))
        self.dll.bb_create.argtypes=[C.c_wchar_p,C.c_void_p,C.c_void_p,C.c_uint64]
        self.dll.bb_create.restype=C.c_void_p
        self.dll.bb_destroy.argtypes=[C.c_void_p]
        self.dll.bb_begin.argtypes=[C.c_void_p,C.c_uint64,C.c_uint32]
        self.dll.bb_begin.restype=C.c_int
        self.dll.bb_set_image.argtypes=[C.c_void_p,C.c_uint64,C.c_uint64,C.c_void_p]
        self.dll.bb_set_image.restype=C.c_int
        for name in ['bb_reason','bb_total','bb_slice','bb_blocks','bb_decodes','bb_hits']:
            method=getattr(self.dll,name);method.argtypes=[C.c_void_p]
            method.restype=C.c_uint32 if name=='bb_reason' else C.c_uint64
        self.read=read or uclib.uc_mem_read
        self.state=self.dll.bb_create(str(Path(capstone.__file__).parent/'lib/capstone.dll'),
                                     C.cast(self.read,C.c_void_p),C.cast(uclib.uc_emu_stop,C.c_void_p),total)
        assert self.state
        self.hook=C.c_uint64()
        error=uclib.uc_hook_add(uc._uch,C.byref(self.hook),U.UC_HOOK_BLOCK,C.cast(self.dll.bb_callback,C.c_void_p),self.state,1,0)
        assert error==0,error
    def begin(self,limit,ms=10000):
        assert self.dll.bb_begin(self.state,limit,ms)
    def get(self,name): return getattr(self.dll,'bb_'+name)(self.state)
    def close(self):
        uclib.uc_hook_del(self.uc._uch,self.hook)
        self.dll.bb_destroy(self.state)
        self.state=None

def machine(code,backing=None):
    uc=U.Uc(U.UC_ARCH_X86,U.UC_MODE_64)
    if backing is None: uc.mem_map(BASE,0x2000)
    else: uc.mem_map_ptr(BASE,0x2000,U.UC_PROT_ALL,C.addressof(backing))
    uc.mem_write(BASE,code)
    uc.reg_write(X.UC_X86_REG_RSP,BASE+0x1f00)
    uc.reg_write(X.UC_X86_REG_EFLAGS,0x202)
    return uc

def registers(uc):
    return tuple(uc.reg_read(r) for r in [X.UC_X86_REG_RAX,X.UC_X86_REG_RCX,X.UC_X86_REG_RSP,X.UC_X86_REG_EFLAGS,X.UC_X86_REG_RIP])

def run_bounded(uc,budget,end,slice_limit):
    pc=uc.reg_read(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP) or BASE
    while pc!=end:
        budget.begin(slice_limit)
        uc.emu_start(pc,end,timeout=0,count=0)
        assert not uc.query(U.UC_QUERY_TIMEOUT),'Emergency timeout: do not resume this state'
        assert budget.get('reason') in [0,1],budget.get('reason')
        pc=uc.reg_read(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP)

def main():
    count=1000
    loop=b'\xb9'+struct.pack('<I',count)+b'\x31\xc0\x83\xc0\x03\xff\xc9\x75\xf9\x90'
    stock=machine(loop); stock.emu_start(BASE,BASE+14,count=1000000)
    for chunk in [1,7,4096]:
        uc=machine(loop); b=BlockBudget(uc,1000000)
        run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+14,chunk)
        assert registers(uc)==registers(stock),(chunk,registers(uc),registers(stock))
        assert b.get('total')==count*3+2,b.get('total')
        b.close()
    # Stop before a block that would exceed the total, without executing it.
    uc=machine(b'\x90\x90\xeb\xfc'); b=BlockBudget(uc,4); b.begin(100)
    uc.emu_start(BASE,BASE+0x100,timeout=1000000)
    assert b.get('reason')==2 and b.get('total')==3
    assert uc.reg_read(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP)==BASE
    b.close()
    # Unicorn presents each REP iteration as a block; preserve its count/semantics.
    rep=b'\x48\xc7\xc1'+struct.pack('<I',500)+b'\x48\xc7\xc7'+struct.pack('<I',BASE+0x1000)+b'\xb0\x55\xf3\xaa\x90'
    stock=machine(rep); stock.emu_start(BASE,BASE+len(rep)-1,count=10000)
    uc=machine(rep); b=BlockBudget(uc,10000)
    run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+len(rep)-1,20)
    assert registers(uc)==registers(stock)
    assert bytes(uc.mem_read(BASE+0x1000,500))==b'\x55'*500
    assert b.get('total')==504
    b.close()
    # Deadline is checked synchronously, never changing a guest register.
    uc=machine(b'\xeb\xfe'); b=BlockBudget(uc,10**12); b.begin(10**12,10)
    start=time.perf_counter(); uc.emu_start(BASE,BASE+0x100,timeout=1000000)
    assert b.get('reason')==3 and time.perf_counter()-start<1
    b.close()
    for direct in [False,True]:
        # Same address and size, changed bytes/count: never reuse a stale cache.
        backing=C.create_string_buffer(0x2000) if direct else None
        uc=machine(b'\xb8\x01\0\0\0\xeb\0',backing); b=BlockBudget(uc,100)
        if direct: assert b.dll.bb_set_image(b.state,BASE,len(backing),C.addressof(backing))
        run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+7,100); assert b.get('total')==2
        uc.mem_write(BASE,b'\x90'*5+b'\xeb\0'); uc.reg_write(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP,BASE)
        run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+7,100)
        assert b.get('total')==8 and b.get('decodes')==2
        b.close()
        # Actual guest SMC, compare with stock count using both read paths.
        smc=b'\xc6\x05\x02\0\0\0\x04\x83\xc0\x01\x90'
        stock=machine(smc); stock.emu_start(BASE,BASE+10,count=100)
        backing=C.create_string_buffer(0x2000) if direct else None
        uc=machine(smc,backing); b=BlockBudget(uc,100)
        if direct: assert b.dll.bb_set_image(b.state,BASE,len(backing),C.addressof(backing))
        run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+10,100)
        assert registers(uc)==registers(stock) and uc.reg_read(X.UC_X86_REG_RAX)==4
        if direct: assert backing[9]==b'\x04'
        b.close()
    # Direct reads require whole-block containment. Count fallback reads instead
    # of inferring the selected path from equivalent output bytes.
    read_type=C.CFUNCTYPE(C.c_int,C.c_void_p,C.c_uint64,C.c_void_p,C.c_size_t)
    for view_offset,view_size,clear,expected_reads in [(0x100,2,False,0),(0x101,1,False,1),
            (0x100,1,False,1),(0x110,2,False,1),(0x100,2,True,1)]:
        backing=C.create_string_buffer(0x2000)
        uc=machine(b'',backing); uc.mem_write(BASE+0x100,b'\x90\x90')
        reads=[]
        @read_type
        def read(engine,address,destination,size):
            reads.append((address,size))
            return uclib.uc_mem_read(engine,address,C.cast(destination,C.POINTER(C.c_char)),size)
        b=BlockBudget(uc,100,read)
        assert b.dll.bb_set_image(b.state,BASE+view_offset,view_size,C.addressof(backing)+view_offset)
        # Invalid updates must reject and preserve the existing borrowed view.
        for base,size,pointer in [(BASE,0,C.addressof(backing)),(BASE,2,None),
                (2**64-1,2,C.addressof(backing)),(BASE,2,2**64-1)]:
            assert not b.dll.bb_set_image(b.state,base,size,pointer)
        assert not b.dll.bb_set_image(None,BASE,2,C.addressof(backing))
        if clear: assert b.dll.bb_set_image(b.state,0,0,None)
        uc.reg_write(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP,BASE+0x100)
        run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+0x102,100)
        assert len(reads)==expected_reads,(view_offset,view_size,clear,reads)
        assert b.get('total')==2
        b.close()
    # External API hook can stop mid-block. Charge remains conservative.
    uc=machine(b'\x90\x90\x90\xeb\x00'); b=BlockBudget(uc,100)
    def stop_api(uc,address,size,data): uc.emu_stop()
    hook=uc.hook_add(U.UC_HOOK_CODE,stop_api,begin=BASE+1,end=BASE+1)
    b.begin(100); uc.emu_start(BASE,BASE+5,count=0)
    assert uc.reg_read(X.UC_X86_REG_RIP)==BASE+1 and b.get('total')>=1
    uc.hook_del(hook); run_bounded(uc,b,BASE+5,100);b.close()
    # Invalid dependencies fail closed before attaching a callback.
    assert not b.dll.bb_create('capstone.dll',C.cast(uclib.uc_mem_read,C.c_void_p),C.cast(uclib.uc_emu_stop,C.c_void_p),100)
    assert not b.dll.bb_create('C:/Windows/System32/kernel32.dll',C.cast(uclib.uc_mem_read,C.c_void_p),C.cast(uclib.uc_emu_stop,C.c_void_p),100)
    print('PASS: stock register/flag parity, REP, bounds, deadlines, SMC, direct image boundaries, API stops and dependencies')

if __name__=='__main__': main()

```

`tests/test_host_image.py`:

```py
"""Host-backed main-image coherence, native scheduler lifetime and restore tests."""
import ctypes as c
import gc
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace
import unittest
import weakref
import zipfile

import unicorn as u
from unicorn import x86_const as x

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'standalone'))
from native_support import HostImage, BlockBudget
from emulate import Emulator, Unsupported
from work_files import work_directory

BASE=0x400000
SIZE=8192
OUTSIDE=0x800000

class HostImageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.uc=u.Uc(u.UC_ARCH_X86,u.UC_MODE_64)
        self.image=HostImage(self.uc,BASE,SIZE)
        self.image.map_region(BASE,SIZE)
        self.uc.mem_map(OUTSIDE,4096)

    def make_budget(self):
        budget=BlockBudget(self.uc,10000,image=self.image)
        self.addCleanup(budget.close)
        return budget

    def run_guest(self,budget,start,end):
        budget.begin(10000,1000)
        self.uc.emu_start(start,end,count=0)
        self.assertEqual(budget.get('reason'),0)

    def test_guest_smc_and_host_api_writes_keep_backing_and_cache_coherent(self):
        code=b'\xb8\x01\0\0\0\x90\x90\x90'
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,code)
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(self.image.pointer,8),code)
        budget=self.make_budget()
        self.run_guest(budget,BASE,BASE+8)
        self.assertEqual(self.uc.reg_read(x.UC_X86_REG_EAX),1)
        self.assertEqual(budget.get('total'),4)
        # The writer executes outside the host image and uses fallback reads.
        writer=b'\x48\xbf'+struct.pack('<Q',BASE+1)+b'\xc7\x07\x02\0\0\0'
        writer+=b'\xe9'+struct.pack('<i',BASE-(OUTSIDE+len(writer)+5))
        self.uc.mem_write(OUTSIDE,writer)
        self.run_guest(budget,OUTSIDE,BASE+8)
        self.assertEqual(self.uc.reg_read(x.UC_X86_REG_EAX),2)
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(self.image.pointer+1,4),b'\x02\0\0\0')
        changed=b'\x31\xc0'+b'\x90'*6
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,changed)
        before=budget.get('total')
        self.run_guest(budget,BASE,BASE+8)
        self.assertEqual(budget.get('total')-before,7)
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(self.image.pointer,8),changed)

    def test_cross_boundary_block_uses_fallback(self):
        self.uc.mem_map(BASE+SIZE,4096)
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE+SIZE-4,b'\x90'*8)
        budget=self.make_budget()
        self.run_guest(budget,BASE+SIZE-4,BASE+SIZE+4)
        self.assertEqual(budget.get('total'),8)

    def test_snapshot_header_normalization_never_mutates_live_backing(self):
        live=b'\x55'*SIZE
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,live)
        emu=self.emulator_shell()
        emu.base=BASE; emu.size=SIZE
        section=SimpleNamespace(Misc_VirtualSize=100,VirtualAddress=4096,get_file_offset=lambda:64)
        emu.pe=SimpleNamespace(sections=[section])
        snapshot=emu.image()
        self.assertEqual(struct.unpack_from('<II',snapshot,80),(4096,4096))
        snapshot[0]=0
        self.assertEqual(bytes(self.uc.mem_read(BASE,SIZE)),live)
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(self.image.pointer,SIZE),live)

    def test_engine_and_budget_pin_original_aligned_allocation(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.image.pointer%4096,0)
        raw=c.addressof(self.image._buffer)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.image.pointer,raw)
        self.assertLessEqual(self.image.pointer+SIZE,raw+len(self.image._buffer))
        backing_ref=weakref.ref(self.image._buffer)
        image_ref=weakref.ref(self.image)
        budget=self.make_budget()
        del self.image
        gc.collect()
        self.assertIsNotNone(image_ref())
        self.assertIsNotNone(backing_ref())
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,b'\x90')
        self.run_guest(budget,BASE,BASE+1)
        budget.close()
        budget.image=None
        gc.collect()
        self.assertIsNone(image_ref())
        self.assertIsNotNone(backing_ref())
        # Keeping only UC remains safe: its backing reference has no UC cycle.
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,b'\x90')
        self.uc.emu_start(BASE,BASE+1,count=1)

    def checkpoint(self,directory,regions):
        path=Path(directory)/'state.zip'
        state={'regions':regions,'registers':{},'handles':{}}
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path,'w') as archive:
            archive.writestr('state.json',json.dumps(state))
            archive.writestr('written.bin',b'\1'*SIZE)
            for begin,end,perm in regions:
                payload=bytearray(end-begin+1)
                if begin==BASE: payload[:8]=b'\xb8\x03\0\0\0\x90\x90\x90'
                archive.writestr(hex(begin),payload)
        return path

    def emulator_shell(self):
        emu=Emulator.__new__(Emulator)
        emu.uc=self.uc
        emu.host_image=self.image
        emu.written=bytearray(SIZE)
        return emu

    def test_split_checkpoint_restore_preserves_pointer_permissions_and_execution(self):
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,b'old bytes')
        pointer=self.image.pointer
        regions=[(BASE,BASE+4095,u.UC_PROT_READ|u.UC_PROT_EXEC),
                 (BASE+4096,BASE+SIZE-1,u.UC_PROT_READ|u.UC_PROT_WRITE),
                 (OUTSIDE,OUTSIDE+4095,u.UC_PROT_ALL)]
        emu=self.emulator_shell()
        with work_directory(prefix='vmp-host-image-') as directory:
            emu.restore(self.checkpoint(directory,regions))
        self.assertEqual(self.image.pointer,pointer)
        self.assertEqual(list(self.uc.mem_regions()),regions)
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(pointer,SIZE),bytes(self.uc.mem_read(BASE,SIZE)))
        self.assertEqual(emu.written,b'\1'*SIZE)
        # Scheduler attaches after restore, as in the CLI.
        budget=self.make_budget()
        self.run_guest(budget,BASE,BASE+8)
        self.assertEqual(self.uc.reg_read(x.UC_X86_REG_EAX),3)
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE+4096,b'restored')
        self.assertEqual(c.string_at(pointer+4096,8),b'restored')

    def test_restore_rejects_cross_boundary_and_missing_image_before_unmapping(self):
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE,b'original')
        invalid=[[(BASE-4096,BASE+4095,u.UC_PROT_ALL)],
                 [(BASE,BASE+4095,u.UC_PROT_ALL)]]
        emu=self.emulator_shell()
        for regions in invalid:
            with work_directory(prefix='vmp-host-image-') as directory:
                with self.assertRaises((ValueError,Unsupported)):
                    emu.restore(self.checkpoint(directory,regions))
            self.assertEqual(bytes(self.uc.mem_read(BASE,8)),b'original')
            self.assertEqual(emu.written,bytes(SIZE))

    def test_ranges_and_engine_mismatch_are_rejected(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.image.offset(BASE+4096,4096),4096)
        self.assertIsNone(self.image.offset(OUTSIDE,4096))
        for base,size in [(BASE-4096,8192),(BASE+4096,8192),(-1,4096),(BASE,0),(2**64-1,2)]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError): self.image.offset(base,size)
        for base,size in [(BASE+1,4096),(BASE,4095),(BASE,0),(2**64-4096,8192)]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError): HostImage(self.uc,base,size)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError): self.image.map_region(OUTSIDE,4096)
        other=u.Uc(u.UC_ARCH_X86,u.UC_MODE_64)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError): BlockBudget(other,100,image=self.image)

if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_native_scanner.py`:

```py
"""Native metadata scanner parity, stale hints and malformed-input checks."""
import ctypes as c
import json
from pathlib import Path
import random
import struct
import subprocess
from contextlib import contextmanager
import shutil
import uuid
import time

from verify_samples import ROOT, SAMPLES, VERSIONS, NAMES
from verify_synthetic import image


class Scanner:
    def __init__(self,path=None):
        self.dll=c.CDLL(str(path or ROOT/'dist/resource_scanner.dll'))
        self.dll.resource_scanner_abi.restype=c.c_uint
        assert self.dll.resource_scanner_abi()==1
        self.dll.resource_scanner_scan.argtypes=[c.c_void_p,c.c_size_t,c.c_int,c.c_uint32,c.c_uint32,c.POINTER(c.c_void_p),c.POINTER(c.c_size_t)]
        self.dll.resource_scanner_scan.restype=c.c_int
        self.dll.resource_scanner_scan_timed.argtypes=self.dll.resource_scanner_scan.argtypes[:5]+[c.c_uint32]+self.dll.resource_scanner_scan.argtypes[5:]
        self.dll.resource_scanner_scan_timed.restype=c.c_int
        self.dll.resource_scanner_free.argtypes=[c.c_void_p]
        self.dll.resource_scanner_free.restype=None

    def scan(self,blob,hint=None,timeout_ms=None):
        output=c.c_void_p()
        length=c.c_size_t()
        buffer=c.create_string_buffer(bytes(blob))
        rva,key=hint or (0,0)
        if timeout_ms is None:
            status=self.dll.resource_scanner_scan(buffer,len(blob),bool(hint),rva,key,c.byref(output),c.byref(length))
        else:
            status=self.dll.resource_scanner_scan_timed(buffer,len(blob),bool(hint),rva,key,timeout_ms,c.byref(output),c.byref(length))
        try:
            result=json.loads(c.string_at(output,length.value).decode('utf-8')) if output.value else None
        finally:
            self.dll.resource_scanner_free(output)
        return status,result


@contextmanager
def workspace_temp():
    directory=ROOT/'build'/('native-scanner-test-'+uuid.uuid4().hex)
    directory.mkdir()
    try:
        yield directory
    finally:
        assert directory.resolve().is_relative_to((ROOT/'build').resolve())
        shutil.rmtree(directory)


def cli_manifest(path,tmp,index):
    out=tmp/str(index)
    result=subprocess.run([str(ROOT/'dist/vmp-resource-extractor.exe'),str(path),'-o',str(out)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=20)
    assert result.returncode==0,result.stderr
    manifest=json.loads((out/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    return {'table_rva':int(manifest['table_rva'],16),'key':int(manifest['key'],16),
            'total':sum(r['size'] for r in manifest['resources']),
            'resources':[{**{k:r[k] for k in ['type','name','language','size','codepage']},
                          'data_rva':int(r['data_rva'],16)} for r in manifest['resources']]}


def main(scanner=None):
    scanner=scanner or Scanner()
    inputs=[SAMPLES/'protected'/version/'x64'/name for version in VERSIONS for name in NAMES]
    snapshots=[ROOT/'build'/name for name in ['packaged-final-memory.exe','resource-sample-packaged-memory.exe','pawd-dll-stable-memory.exe']]
    inputs += [path for path in snapshots if path.exists()]
    with workspace_temp() as temp:
        temp=Path(temp)
        assert temp.resolve().is_relative_to((ROOT/'build').resolve())
        for index,path in enumerate(inputs):
            blob=path.read_bytes()
            status,response=scanner.scan(blob)
            assert status==0 and response['hint_used'] is False,(path,status,response)
            expected=cli_manifest(path,temp,index)
            assert response['candidates']==[expected],path
            hint=(expected['table_rva'],expected['key'])
            status,selected=scanner.scan(blob,hint)
            assert status==0 and selected['hint_used'] is True
            assert selected['candidates']==[expected]
            status,fallback=scanner.scan(blob,(hint[0],hint[1]^0x1234abcd))
            assert status==0 and fallback['hint_used'] is False
            assert fallback['candidates']==[expected]
        # Full discovery must retain all candidates; a valid hint is a selection.
        multiple=image(True,[(0x137,0x12345678,True),(0x737,0xabcd1234,False)])
        status,response=scanner.scan(multiple)
        assert status==0 and len(response['candidates'])==2
        status,response=scanner.scan(multiple,(0x1737,0xabcd1234))
        assert status==0 and len(response['candidates'])==1 and response['hint_used']
        assert response['candidates'][0]['table_rva']==0x1737
        # Names and values stay typed across the native JSON boundary.
        status,response=scanner.scan(image(False,[(0x137,0xffffffff,True)]))
        assert status==0 and response['candidates'][0]['resources'][0]['type']=='資料_İ'
        original=(SAMPLES/'originals/x64'/NAMES[0]).read_bytes()
        status,response=scanner.scan(original)
        assert status==1 and response['candidates']==[]
        malformed=[b'',b'MZ',original[:256],bytes(64)]
        rng=random.Random(451)
        malformed += [rng.randbytes(n) for n in [64,128,1024,4096]]
        started=time.perf_counter()
        for blob in malformed:
            status,response=scanner.scan(blob)
            assert status==2 and 'error' in response
        assert time.perf_counter()-started<5
        output=c.c_void_p(123)
        length=c.c_size_t(123)
        assert scanner.dll.resource_scanner_scan(None,1<<40,0,0,0,c.byref(output),c.byref(length))==2
        scanner.dll.resource_scanner_free(output)
        assert scanner.dll.resource_scanner_scan(None,0,0,0,0,None,c.byref(length))==2
        # Deterministic expired deadline, including the hinted path. A timeout
        # response is owned exactly like other results and must not poison reuse.
        valid=image(True,[(0x137,0x12345678,True)])
        started=time.perf_counter()
        for hint in [None,(0x1137,0x12345678)]:
            status,response=scanner.scan(valid,hint,timeout_ms=0)
            assert status==5 and 'time limit' in response['error']
        assert time.perf_counter()-started<1
        # Candidate-heavy contents exercise checks during discovery, not just
        # the immediate-expiry entry guard. The full scan would be much longer.
        heavy=bytearray(valid[:0x200])
        heavy.extend(struct.pack('<IIII',0,0,0,0x80001000)*65536)
        struct.pack_into('<I',heavy,0x188+8,0x100000)
        struct.pack_into('<I',heavy,0x188+16,0x100000)
        started=time.perf_counter()
        status,response=scanner.scan(heavy,timeout_ms=5)
        assert status==5 and 'time limit' in response['error']
        assert time.perf_counter()-started<2
        status,response=scanner.scan(valid)
        assert status==0 and len(response['candidates'])==1
    print(f'PASS: {len(inputs)} CLI/native metadata matches, valid/stale hints, ambiguity, Unicode, malformed inputs, deadlines and ABI ownership')


if __name__=='__main__': main()

```

`tests/test_native_support.py`:

```py
"""Coverage adapter lifetime, checkpoint bitmap, and native hook cleanup checks."""
import ctypes as c
import gc
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys
import unittest
from unittest import mock
import weakref

import unicorn as u

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'standalone'))
from native_support import Coverage, uclib

BASE=0x400000
CODE=0x100000

class Bitmap(bytearray):
    pass

class CoverageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.uc=u.Uc(u.UC_ARCH_X86,u.UC_MODE_64)
        self.uc.mem_map(BASE,4096)
        self.uc.mem_map(CODE,4096)
        self.code=b''.join(b'\x48\xbf'+struct.pack('<Q',BASE+offset)+b'\xc7\x07\0\0\0\0'
                           for offset in (0x10,0x20,0x30))
        self.uc.mem_write(CODE,self.code)
        self.bitmap=Bitmap(4096)
        self.coverage=Coverage(self.uc,BASE,self.bitmap)
        self.addCleanup(self.coverage.close)

    def run_guest(self):
        self.uc.emu_start(CODE,CODE+len(self.code),count=100)

    def test_disjoint_hooks_and_in_place_checkpoint_restore(self):
        self.coverage.attach(BASE+0x10,BASE+0x1f)
        self.coverage.attach(BASE+0x30,BASE+0x3f)
        self.bitmap[:8]=b'\1'*8
        # Host API writes are deliberately not guest initialization evidence.
        self.uc.mem_write(BASE+0x10,b'host')
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],0)
        pointer=c.addressof(self.coverage.view)
        self.run_guest()
        expected=bytearray(4096)
        expected[:8]=b'\1'*8
        expected[0x10:0x14]=expected[0x30:0x34]=b'\1'*4
        self.assertEqual(self.bitmap,expected)
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats(),dict(callbacks=2,writes=2,bytes=8,generation=2,rejected=0))
        self.bitmap[:]=b'\0'*4096
        self.assertEqual(c.addressof(self.coverage.view),pointer)
        self.run_guest()
        expected[:8]=b'\0'*8
        self.assertEqual(self.bitmap,expected)
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],4)

    def test_view_pins_bitmap_and_close_removes_callbacks(self):
        self.coverage.attach(BASE,BASE+4095)
        bitmap_ref=weakref.ref(self.bitmap)
        del self.bitmap
        gc.collect()
        self.assertIsNotNone(bitmap_ref())
        with self.assertRaises(BufferError): bitmap_ref().append(0)
        self.run_guest()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],3)
        self.coverage.close()
        self.coverage.close()
        self.run_guest()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],3)
        self.assertFalse(self.coverage.hooks)
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError): self.coverage.attach(BASE,BASE+1)

    def test_failed_attach_removes_previous_hooks(self):
        self.coverage.attach(BASE,BASE+4095)
        with mock.patch.object(uclib,'uc_hook_add',return_value=u.UC_ERR_ARG):
            with self.assertRaises(u.UcError): self.coverage.attach(BASE,BASE+15)
        self.assertTrue(self.coverage.closed)
        self.assertFalse(self.coverage.hooks)
        self.run_guest()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],0)

    def test_failed_cleanup_retains_only_live_handles_for_retry(self):
        self.coverage.attach(BASE+0x10,BASE+0x1f)
        self.coverage.attach(BASE+0x30,BASE+0x3f)
        retained=self.coverage.hooks[-1]
        delete=uclib.uc_hook_del
        def fail_last(uc,handle):
            return u.UC_ERR_ARG if handle==retained else delete(uc,handle)
        with mock.patch.object(uclib,'uc_hook_del',side_effect=fail_last):
            with self.assertRaises(u.UcError): self.coverage.close()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.hooks,[retained])
        self.assertFalse(self.coverage.closed)
        self.run_guest()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],1)
        self.coverage.close()
        self.assertTrue(self.coverage.closed)
        self.run_guest()
        self.assertEqual(self.coverage.stats()['writes'],1)

    def test_invalid_ranges_never_become_global_hooks(self):
        for begin,end in [(BASE+1,BASE),(BASE-1,BASE),(BASE,BASE+4096)]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError): self.coverage.attach(begin,end)
        for base,bitmap in [(0,bytearray()),(-1,bytearray(1)),(2**64-1,bytearray(2))]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError): Coverage(self.uc,base,bitmap)
        self.assertFalse(self.coverage.hooks)

if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_native_tracker.py`:

```py
"""Native coverage callback boundary, ABI, and live Unicorn equivalence tests."""
import ctypes as c
from pathlib import Path
import random
import struct

import unicorn as u
from unicorn.unicorn_py3.unicorn import uclib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def library():
    lib = c.CDLL(str(ROOT / 'dist/vmp-native-tracker.dll'))
    lib.vmp_tracker_abi_version.restype = c.c_uint
    lib.vmp_tracker_state_size.restype = c.c_size_t
    lib.vmp_tracker_callback.restype = c.c_void_p
    lib.vmp_tracker_init.argtypes = [c.c_void_p, c.c_size_t, c.c_uint64, c.c_uint64, c.c_void_p, c.c_size_t]
    lib.vmp_tracker_init.restype = c.c_int
    lib.vmp_tracker_stats.argtypes = [c.c_void_p, c.c_void_p, c.c_size_t]
    lib.vmp_tracker_stats.restype = c.c_int
    assert lib.vmp_tracker_abi_version() == 1
    return lib

def tracker(lib, base, size):
    bitmap = bytearray(size)
    view = (c.c_uint8 * size).from_buffer(bitmap)
    storage = c.create_string_buffer(lib.vmp_tracker_state_size())
    assert lib.vmp_tracker_init(storage, len(storage), base, size, view, size)
    return bitmap, view, storage

def boundary_test(lib):
    bitmap, view, storage = tracker(lib, 0x1000, 64)
    callback = c.CFUNCTYPE(None,c.c_void_p,c.c_int,c.c_uint64,c.c_int,c.c_int64,c.c_void_p)(lib.vmp_tracker_callback())
    reference = bytearray(64)
    accepted = rejected = count = length = 0
    cases = [(0x1000,1,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),(0x1038,8,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),
             (0xfff,2,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),(0x103f,2,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),
             (0x1000,0,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),(0x1000,-1,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),
             (0xffffffffffffffff,8,u.UC_MEM_WRITE),(0x1004,4,u.UC_MEM_READ)]
    rng = random.Random(112)
    cases += [(rng.randrange(0xff0,0x1050),rng.randrange(-2,25),u.UC_MEM_WRITE) for _ in range(1000)]
    for address,size,access in cases:
        callback(None,access,address,size,0,storage)
        count += 1
        offset = address - 0x1000
        if access == u.UC_MEM_WRITE and size > 0 and 0 <= offset and offset + size <= 64:
            reference[offset:offset+size] = b'\1' * size
            accepted += 1
            length += size
        else:
            rejected += 1
        assert bitmap == reference
    stats = (c.c_uint64 * 5)()
    assert lib.vmp_tracker_stats(storage,stats,5)
    assert list(stats) == [count,accepted,length,accepted,rejected]
    assert not lib.vmp_tracker_init(storage,len(storage),0,65,view,64)
    assert not lib.vmp_tracker_init(storage,len(storage),0xffffffffffffffff,2,view,64)
    assert not lib.vmp_tracker_init(storage,len(storage)-1,0,64,view,64)
    assert not lib.vmp_tracker_init(None,len(storage),0,64,view,64)
    # Last address of the 64-bit address space is valid for a one-byte range.
    assert lib.vmp_tracker_init(storage,len(storage),0xffffffffffffffff,1,view,64)
    callback(None,u.UC_MEM_WRITE,0xffffffffffffffff,1,0,storage)
    assert bitmap[0] == 1

def unicorn_test(lib):
    base = 0x400000
    native, view, storage = tracker(lib,base,4096)
    reference = bytearray(4096)
    uc = u.Uc(u.UC_ARCH_X86,u.UC_MODE_64)
    uc.mem_map(0x100000,4096)
    uc.mem_map(base,4096)
    # MOV RDI, base+3; MOV RCX, 257; MOV AL,0; REP STOSB; MOV [RDI],RDI.
    # Zero-valued writes count as initialized bytes, as do overlapping writes.
    code = b'\x48\xbf'+struct.pack('<Q',base+3)+b'\x48\xc7\xc1\x01\x01\0\0\xb0\0\xf3\xaa\x48\x89\x3f'
    uc.mem_write(0x100000,code)
    native_hook = c.c_size_t()
    error = uclib.uc_hook_add(uc._uch,c.byref(native_hook),u.UC_HOOK_MEM_WRITE,
                             lib.vmp_tracker_callback(),c.cast(storage,c.c_void_p),
                             c.c_uint64(base),c.c_uint64(base+4095))
    assert error == 0,error
    def python_write(uc,access,address,size,value,data):
        offset = address-base
        if 0 <= offset and offset+size <= len(reference):
            reference[offset:offset+size] = b'\1'*size
    python_hook = uc.hook_add(u.UC_HOOK_MEM_WRITE,python_write,begin=base,end=base+4095)
    try:
        uc.emu_start(0x100000,0x100000+len(code),count=1000)
        assert native == reference
        assert native[:3] == b'\0'*3
        assert native[3:268] == b'\1'*265
        assert native[268:] == b'\0'*(4096-268)
        stats = (c.c_uint64*5)()
        assert lib.vmp_tracker_stats(storage,stats,5)
        assert stats[1] == 258 and stats[2] == 265 and stats[4] == 0
    finally:
        uc.hook_del(python_hook)
        assert uclib.uc_hook_del(uc._uch,native_hook.value) == 0

def main():
    lib = library()
    boundary_test(lib)
    unicorn_test(lib)
    print('PASS: native ABI, randomized byte coverage, overflow/bounds rejection, live Unicorn callback equivalence')

if __name__ == '__main__': main()

```

`tests/test_plugin_backend.py`:

```py
"""Exercise runtime extraction through the plugin's exact backend and a memory reader."""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import sys

import pefile
from verify_samples import ROOT, SAMPLES, VERSIONS, NAMES, original, work_directory
from verify_synthetic import image

EXE=ROOT/'build/plugin-backend-tests.exe'

def mapped(blob):
    pe=pefile.PE(data=blob)
    result=bytearray(pe.OPTIONAL_HEADER.SizeOfImage)
    data=pe.get_memory_mapped_image()
    result[:len(data)]=data
    pe.close()
    return result

def synthetic(tables):
    blob=image(True,tables)
    struct.pack_into('<I',blob,0x98+56,0x3000)
    return mapped(blob)

def run(memory,output,mode='normal',value=0,ok=True,base=0x7ff612340000):
    result=subprocess.run([str(EXE),str(memory),str(output),hex(base),mode,str(value)],
                          text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=30)
    assert (result.returncode==0)==ok,result.stdout+result.stderr
    return result

def extracted(directory):
    metadata=json.loads((directory/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    assert metadata['method']=='x64dbg-memory'
    actual={}
    for resource in metadata['resources']:
        key=(resource['type'],resource['name'],resource['language'])
        assert key not in actual
        actual[key]=((directory/resource['file']).read_bytes(),resource['codepage'])
    return metadata,actual

def main():
    count=0
    with work_directory(prefix='plugin-backend-',root=ROOT/'build') as temp:
        temp=Path(temp)
        memory=temp/'memory.bin'
        for version in VERSIONS:
            for name in NAMES:
                input=SAMPLES/'protected'/version/'x64'/name
                before=hashlib.sha256(input.read_bytes()).digest()
                memory.write_bytes(mapped(input.read_bytes()))
                output=temp/(version+'-'+name)
                run(memory,output,'freeze')
                metadata,actual=extracted(output)
                expected={k:v for k,v in original(SAMPLES/'originals/x64'/name).items() if k[0]!=24}
                assert actual==expected,(version,name)
                assert metadata['module_base']=='0x7ff612340000'
                assert hashlib.sha256(input.read_bytes()).digest()==before
                count+=len(actual)
        # The snapshot owns its bytes: freeze mode destroys the reader's source
        # before extraction, and all 126 resources above still match originals.
        fixture=synthetic([(0x137,0x12345678,True)])
        memory.write_bytes(fixture)
        output=temp/'unicode'
        run(memory,output)
        metadata,actual=extracted(output)
        assert actual=={('資料_İ',543,1041):(b'abc\0xyz',65001)}
        saved={p.name:p.read_bytes() for p in output.iterdir()}
        run(memory,output,ok=False)
        assert {p.name:p.read_bytes() for p in output.iterdir()}==saved
        empty=temp/'empty'; empty.mkdir()
        run(memory,empty,ok=False)
        assert empty.is_dir() and not list(empty.iterdir())
        for mode,value,part in [('blocked',0x1000,'Cannot read'),('cancel',2,'state changed'),
                                ('changed',0,'headers changed'),('early-change',0,'headers changed'),
                                ('select',99,'Select a resource table')]:
            target=temp/mode
            result=run(memory,target,mode,value,ok=False)
            assert part in result.stderr,result.stderr
            assert not target.exists()
        run(memory,temp/'overflow',ok=False,base=0xfffffffffffff000)
        # All candidates are preserved, including explicit selection of a second tree.
        memory.write_bytes(synthetic([(0x137,0x12345678,True),(0x737,0xabcd1234,False)]))
        assert 'candidates=2' in run(memory,temp/'multiple','list').stdout
        run(memory,temp/'selected','select',1)
        assert extracted(temp/'selected')[0]['table_rva']=='0x1737'
        bad=bytearray(fixture)
        struct.pack_into('<H',bad,0x84,0x14c)
        memory.write_bytes(bad)
        assert 'x64 modules only' in run(memory,temp/'x32',ok=False).stderr
        assert not list(temp.glob('*.partial-vre-*'))
    print(f'PASS: 21 mapped fixtures / {count} resources, immutable snapshots, Unicode, candidate selection, inaccessible pages, cancellation, changed headers, overflow, overwrite and cleanup')

if __name__=='__main__': main()

```

`tests/test_timeout_state.py`:

```py
"""A Unicorn timeout must never produce resumable execution state."""
from collections import Counter, deque
from contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'standalone'))
import emulate
from work_files import work_directory


class FakeUnicorn:
    def __init__(self,timeout=True,raises=False):
        self.timeout=timeout
        self.raises=raises
        self.starts=0

    def emu_start(self,*args,**kwargs):
        self.starts+=1
        if self.raises:
            raise emulate.u.UcError(emulate.u.UC_ERR_EXCEPTION)

    def query(self,query):
        assert query==emulate.u.UC_QUERY_TIMEOUT
        return self.timeout

    def emu_stop(self): pass
    def mem_read(self,address,size): return bytes(size)


class FakeBudget:
    def __init__(self,*args): self.closed=False
    def begin(self,*args): pass
    def get(self,name): return 1000000 if name=='total' else 0
    def stats(self): return {'total':1000000}
    def close(self): self.closed=True


def machine(timeout=True,raises=False):
    # Isolate scheduling from PE setup, native libraries and resource scanning.
    emu=emulate.Emulator.__new__(emulate.Emulator)
    emu.uc=FakeUnicorn(timeout,raises)
    emu.reason=None
    emu.nonresumable_reason=None
    emu.ready=False
    emu.steps=0
    emu.scanner=None
    emu.native_coverage=None
    emu.calls=Counter()
    emu.events=[]
    emu.blocks=deque()
    emu.metrics={'emulation_seconds':0.0,'scan_seconds':0.0,'snapshot_seconds':0.0,'scans':0}
    emu.reg=lambda name: 0x100000
    emu.md=SimpleNamespace(disasm=lambda *args: [])
    emu.dump=lambda path: Path(path).write_bytes(b'diagnostic guest memory')
    return emu


class TimeoutStateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_timeout_is_terminal_even_when_emu_start_raises(self):
        for scheduler in ['stock','block']:
            for raises in [False,True]:
                with self.subTest(scheduler=scheduler,raises=raises), work_directory(root=ROOT/'build') as tmp:
                    emu=machine(raises=raises)
                    budget=FakeBudget()
                    with patch.object(emulate,'BlockBudget',return_value=budget), redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                        result=emu.run(1000000,1,Path(tmp)/'memory.exe',scheduler)
                    self.assertEqual(emu.uc.starts,1)
                    self.assertFalse(result['resumable'])
                    self.assertIn('Unicorn execution timeout',result['nonresumable_reason'])
                    self.assertFalse(emu.ready)
                    if raises:
                        self.assertIn(str(emulate.u.UcError(emulate.u.UC_ERR_EXCEPTION)),result['stop'])
                    if scheduler=='block': self.assertTrue(budget.closed)
                    checkpoint=Path(tmp)/'unsafe.checkpoint.zip'
                    with self.assertRaisesRegex(emulate.Unsupported,'Checkpoint refused'):
                        emu.checkpoint(checkpoint)
                    self.assertFalse(checkpoint.exists())
                    # Clearing a stop reason must not bypass the terminal flag.
                    emu.reason=None
                    with self.assertRaisesRegex(emulate.Unsupported,'Cannot resume emulation'):
                        emu.run(1000000,1,Path(tmp)/'again.exe',scheduler)
                    self.assertEqual(emu.uc.starts,1)

    def test_synchronous_budget_stop_remains_resumable(self):
        emu=machine(timeout=False)
        with work_directory(root=ROOT/'build') as tmp, redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
            result=emu.run(1000000,1,Path(tmp)/'memory.exe')
        self.assertTrue(result['resumable'])
        self.assertIsNone(result['nonresumable_reason'])

    def test_cli_skips_checkpoint_but_publishes_diagnostics(self):
        for raises in [False,True]:
            with self.subTest(raises=raises), work_directory(root=ROOT/'build') as tmp:
                folder=Path(tmp)
                source=folder/'sample.exe'
                source.write_bytes(b'fixture input')
                snapshot=folder/'memory.exe'
                emu=machine(raises=raises)
                stderr=io.StringIO()
                argv=['emulate.py',str(source),'--snapshot',str(snapshot),'--checkpoint','--scheduler','stock']
                with patch.object(sys,'argv',argv), patch.object(emulate,'Emulator',return_value=emu), redirect_stderr(stderr), redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                    status=emulate.main()
                self.assertEqual(status,1)
                self.assertEqual(emu.uc.starts,1)
                self.assertEqual(snapshot.read_bytes(),b'diagnostic guest memory')
                report=json.loads(snapshot.with_suffix('.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
                self.assertFalse(report['resumable'])
                self.assertIn('Unicorn execution timeout',report['checkpoint_skipped'])
                self.assertTrue(report['input_unchanged'])
                self.assertFalse(snapshot.with_suffix('.checkpoint.zip').exists())
                self.assertIn('Checkpoint skipped:',stderr.getvalue())


if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

```

`tests/verify_emulation_models.py`:

```py
"""Regression tests for the API semantics exercised by the packed fixture."""
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'standalone'))
from emulate import Emulator, x
from verify_samples import SAMPLES

def main():
    path=SAMPLES/'originals/x64/ResourcePaths-x64.vmp.exe'
    e=Emulator(path)
    scratch=e.alloc(0x1000)
    sp=e.stack+0x180000
    def call(name,args):
        e.reason=None
        e.uc.reg_write(x.UC_X86_REG_RSP,sp)
        e.q(sp,e.base)
        for register,value in zip(['rcx','rdx','r8','r9'],list(args)+[0]*4):
            e.uc.reg_write(getattr(x,'UC_X86_REG_'+register.upper()),value)
        for i in range(4,10): e.q(sp+0x28+(i-4)*8,args[i] if i<len(args) else 0)
        address=next(a for a,n in e.apis.items() if n==name)
        e.api(e.uc,address,1,None)
        assert not e.reason,e.reason
        return e.reg('rax')
    e.q(scratch,0xfeed)
    assert call('NtQueryInformationProcess',[-1,30,scratch,8,scratch])==0xc0000353
    assert e.readq(scratch)==8,'ReturnLength must be written last; failed query must retain buffer'
    assert call('NtQueryInformationProcess',[-1,30,scratch+1,8,0])==0x80000002
    assert call('NtQueryInformationProcess',[-1,30,scratch,8,1])==0xc0000005
    assert e.readq(scratch)==8
    assert call('NtQueryInformationProcess',[-1,7,scratch,8,0])==0
    assert e.readq(scratch)==0
    assert call('NtSetInformationThread',[-2,17,0,0])==0
    assert call('NtQueryInformationThread',[-2,17,scratch,1,0])==0
    assert bytes(e.uc.mem_read(scratch,1))==b'\1'
    assert call('NtCreateDebugObject',[scratch,8,0,0])==0
    handle=e.readq(scratch)
    assert call('NtQueryObject',[handle,2,0,0,scratch+0x100])==0xc0000004
    assert struct.unpack('<I',e.uc.mem_read(scratch+0x100,4))[0]==0x80
    assert call('NtQueryObject',[handle,2,scratch+0x200,0x80,scratch+0x208])==0
    assert struct.unpack('<I',e.uc.mem_read(scratch+0x208,4))[0]==0x80
    assert call('NtClose',[handle])==0
    assert call('NtClose',[handle])==0xc0000008
    filehandle=e.handle('input-file')
    assert call('NtCreateSection',[scratch,4,0,0,2,0x8000000,filehandle])==0
    section=e.readq(scratch)
    assert e.handles[section]=='input-raw-section'
    e.q(scratch+0x100,0); e.q(scratch+0x108,0)
    assert call('NtMapViewOfSection',[section,-1,scratch+0x100,0,0,0,scratch+0x108,1,0,2])==0
    address=e.readq(scratch+0x100)
    assert bytes(e.uc.mem_read(address,0x1000))==path.read_bytes()[:0x1000]
    assert len(e.written)==e.size
    print('PASS: overlapping outputs, alignment, invalid pointers, thread state, object lifetime and raw file mappings')
    dll_path=path.with_name('pawdResourceFixture-x64.dll')
    if dll_path.exists():
        dll=Emulator(dll_path)
        assert dll.reg('rcx')==dll.base and dll.reg('rdx')==1 and dll.reg('r8')==0
        for address in dll.modules.values():
            assert not dll.base<=address<dll.base+dll.size
        assert dll.modules['ntdll.dll']!=dll.base
        print('PASS: DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH arguments and DLL/system-module address collision')

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```
