`tests/verify_packed_output.py`:

```py
"""Verify an emulated extraction against the original fixture, independently of the extractor."""
import hashlib
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from verify_samples import SAMPLES, original

def main():
    root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('name',nargs='?',default='ResourcePaths-x64',choices=['ResourcePaths-x64','ResourceSample-x64','pawdResourceFixture-x64'])
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    is_dll=args.name=='pawdResourceFixture-x64'
    sample=SAMPLES/'originals/x64'/(args.name+('.dll' if is_dll else '.vmp.exe'))
    output=args.output or Path(str(sample)+'.resources')
    m=json.loads((output/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    original_name='ResourceFixture-x64.dll' if is_dll else args.name+'.exe'
    expected={k:v for k,v in original(sample.with_name(original_name)).items() if k[0]!=24}
    actual={(r['type'],r['name'],r['language']):((output/r['file']).read_bytes(),r['codepage']) for r in m['resources']}
    assert actual==expected
    assert m['method']=='unicorn-emulation'
    assert m['input_sha256']==hashlib.sha256(sample.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    report={'input':sample.name,'input_sha256':m['input_sha256'],'resources':len(actual),
            'bytes':sum(len(v[0]) for v in actual.values()),'matches_original':True,
            'elapsed_seconds':m['emulation_seconds'],
            'files':{r['file']:hashlib.sha256((output/r['file']).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for r in m['resources']}}
    (root/'build'/('packed-verification-'+args.name+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__': main()

```

`tests/verify_plugin_output.py`:

```py
"""Compare an x64dbg extraction with the original PE resource tree (requires pefile)."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path, PureWindowsPath

from verify_samples import original


def require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def unique_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        require(key not in result, f'Duplicate JSON property: {key}')
        result[key] = value
    return result


def verify(original_path, output):
    original_path = Path(original_path).resolve(strict=True)
    output = Path(output).resolve(strict=True)
    manifest_path = output / 'manifest.json'
    require(manifest_path.resolve(strict=True).parent == output,
            'Manifest resolves outside the output directory')
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'),
                          object_pairs_hook=unique_object)
    require(manifest.get('method') == 'x64dbg-memory', 'Not an x64dbg memory extraction')
    require(manifest.get('format') == 'vmprotect-resource-extractor-1', 'Unexpected format')
    require(manifest.get('architecture') == 'x64', 'Unexpected module architecture')
    require(isinstance(manifest.get('module_base'), str), 'Missing module base')
    require(0 < int(manifest['module_base'], 16) < 2**64, 'Invalid module base')
    require(isinstance(manifest.get('resources'), list), 'Missing resource records')

    # Ordinary PE manifests are not part of VMProtect's encrypted resource tree.
    expected = {key: value for key, value in original(original_path).items() if key[0] != 24}
    require(expected, 'Original contains no resources to compare')
    actual, files, hashes = {}, set(), {}
    for item in manifest['resources']:
        key = (item['type'], item['name'], item['language'])
        require(all(type(ident) in (int, str) for ident in key), 'Invalid resource identifier')
        require(key not in actual, f'Duplicate resource identifier: {key!r}')
        filename = item['file']
        require(isinstance(filename, str) and filename, 'Invalid resource filename')
        windows_name = PureWindowsPath(filename)
        require(not windows_name.drive and not windows_name.root and
                len(windows_name.parts) == 1 and filename not in ('.', '..') and
                '/' not in filename and '\\' not in filename and ':' not in filename,
                f'Unsafe resource filename: {filename!r}')
        require(filename.casefold() not in files, f'Duplicate resource file: {filename}')
        files.add(filename.casefold())
        resource_path = (output / filename).resolve(strict=True)
        require(resource_path.parent == output and resource_path.is_file(),
                f'Resource resolves outside output or is not a file: {filename}')
        require(type(item['size']) is int and item['size'] >= 0,
                f'Invalid size: {filename}')
        require(type(item['codepage']) is int and 0 <= item['codepage'] <= 0xffffffff,
                f'Invalid codepage: {filename}')
        data = resource_path.read_bytes()
        require(len(data) == item['size'], f'Size mismatch: {filename}')
        actual[key] = (data, item['codepage'])
        hashes[filename] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

    require(set(actual) == set(expected),
            f'Resource identifiers differ: missing={set(expected) - set(actual)!r}, '
            f'extra={set(actual) - set(expected)!r}')
    for key, value in expected.items():
        require(actual[key] == value, f'Payload or codepage mismatch for {key!r}')
    return {'original': str(original_path), 'output': str(output),
            'method': manifest['method'], 'module_base': manifest['module_base'],
            'resources': len(actual), 'bytes': sum(len(value[0]) for value in actual.values()),
            'matches_original': True, 'files': hashes}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('original', type=Path, help='Unprotected original EXE or DLL')
    parser.add_argument('outputs', type=Path, nargs='+', help='One or more extraction directories')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    for output in args.outputs:
        print(json.dumps(verify(args.original, output), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`tests/verify_samples.py`:

```py
"""Integration checks against the local research fixtures. Requires pefile."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import pefile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'standalone'))
from work_files import work_directory
EXE = ROOT / 'dist/vmp-resource-extractor.exe'
SAMPLES = Path(os.environ.get('VRE_SAMPLES', str(ROOT.parent / 'samples/resource_protection')))
VERSIONS = ['3.3.1', '3.4.0', '3.5.0', '3.6.0', '3.8.0', '3.9.4', '3.10.1']
NAMES = ['ResourceSample-x64.exe', 'ResourcePaths-x64.exe', 'ResourceFixture-x64.dll']

def original(path):
    pe = pefile.PE(str(path))
    out = {}
    def walk(directory, keys=()):
        for entry in directory.entries:
            ident = str(entry.name) if entry.name is not None else entry.id
            current = keys + (ident,)
            if hasattr(entry, 'directory'):
                walk(entry.directory, current)
            else:
                leaf = entry.data.struct
                out[current] = (pe.get_data(leaf.OffsetToData, leaf.Size), leaf.CodePage)
    walk(pe.DIRECTORY_ENTRY_RESOURCE)
    pe.close()
    return out

def run(*args, ok=True):
    result = subprocess.run([str(EXE), *map(str, args)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert (result.returncode == 0) == ok, result.stdout + result.stderr
    return result

def main():
    total = 0
    with work_directory(prefix='vmp-resource-tests-') as tmp:
        tmp = Path(tmp)
        for version in VERSIONS:
            start = time.perf_counter()
            count = 0
            for name in NAMES:
                src = SAMPLES / 'protected' / version / 'x64' / name
                expected = original(SAMPLES / 'originals/x64' / name)
                # The normal PE manifest remains outside VMProtect's private tree.
                expected = {k: v for k, v in expected.items() if k[0] != 24}
                before = hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).digest()
                dest = tmp / (version + '-' + name)
                run(src, '-o', dest)
                manifest = json.loads((dest / 'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
                actual = {}
                for item in manifest['resources']:
                    key = (item['type'], item['name'], item['language'])
                    data = (dest / item['file']).read_bytes()
                    assert len(data) == item['size']
                    actual[key] = (data, item['codepage'])
                assert actual == expected, (version, name)
                assert hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).digest() == before
                snapshot = {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in dest.iterdir()}
                run(src, '-o', dest, ok=False)
                assert snapshot == {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in dest.iterdir()}
                count += len(actual)
            total += count
            print(f'{version}: 3 files, {count} resources verified ({time.perf_counter()-start:.3f}s)')
        plain = SAMPLES / 'originals/x64' / NAMES[0]
        run(plain, '-o', tmp / 'plain', ok=False)
        assert not (tmp / 'plain').exists()
        broken = tmp / 'truncated.exe'
        broken.write_bytes(plain.read_bytes()[:256])
        run(broken, ok=False)
        src = SAMPLES / 'protected/3.3.1/x64' / NAMES[0]
        run(src, '--table-rva', '0x81ddb', '--key', '0', ok=False)
        run(src, '--table-rva', '0x81ddb', ok=False)
    print(f'PASS: 21 protected files, {total} resources; overwrite, input integrity and invalid-input checks')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`tests/verify_snapshot_io.py`:

```py
"""Windows mapped-reader regression: never truncate a published snapshot."""
import mmap
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading

sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'standalone'))
from emulate import atomic_snapshot
from work_files import work_directory

def main():
    with work_directory(prefix='vmp-snapshot-test-') as tmp:
        path=Path(tmp)/'snapshot.bin'
        path.write_bytes(b'previous snapshot')
        with path.open('rb') as f:
            mapped=mmap.mmap(f.fileno(),0,access=mmap.ACCESS_READ)
        try:
            try:
                atomic_snapshot(path,b'new snapshot')
            except OSError:
                assert path.read_bytes()==b'previous snapshot'
            else:
                raise AssertionError('Expected the held Windows mapping to deny replacement')
            assert mapped[:]==b'previous snapshot'
            assert list(path.parent.iterdir())==[path]
            release=threading.Timer(0.25,mapped.close)
            release.start()
            try:
                atomic_snapshot(path,b'new snapshot')
            finally:
                release.join()
            assert path.read_bytes()==b'new snapshot'
            assert list(path.parent.iterdir())==[path]
        finally:
            mapped.close()
    print('PASS: mapped reader preserves the old snapshot; transient locks retry; no partial files remain')

if __name__=='__main__': main()

```

`tests/verify_synthetic.py`:

```py
"""Independent format fixtures: arbitrary keys, unaligned tables, Unicode and ambiguity."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
from verify_samples import run, work_directory

def rol(key, i):
    i &= 31
    return ((key << i) | (key >> ((32-i) & 31))) & 0xffffffff

def image(x64, tables):
    data = bytearray(0x2200)
    def w16(p, v): struct.pack_into('<H', data, p, v)
    def w32(p, v): struct.pack_into('<I', data, p, v)
    data[:2] = b'MZ'
    w32(0x3c, 0x80)
    data[0x80:0x84] = b'PE\0\0'
    w16(0x84, 0x8664 if x64 else 0x14c)
    w16(0x86, 1)
    w16(0x94, 0xf0 if x64 else 0xe0)
    opt = 0x98
    w16(opt, 0x20b if x64 else 0x10b)
    w32(opt+60, 0x200)
    sec = opt + (0xf0 if x64 else 0xe0)
    data[sec:sec+8] = b'.private'
    for off, value in [(8,0x2000),(12,0x1000),(16,0x2000),(20,0x200)]:
        w32(sec+off, value)
    for table_offset, key, named in tables:
        base = 0x200 + table_offset
        def enc(offset, value): w32(base+offset, value ^ key)
        # type -> name -> language -> leaf, deliberately unrelated to research IDs.
        for offset, value in {0:int(named),4:int(not named),8:0x80000060 if named else 123,
                              12:0x80000010,16:0,20:1,24:543,28:0x80000020,
                              32:0,36:1,40:1041,44:48,
                              48:0x1000+table_offset+0xc0,52:7,56:65001,60:0}.items():
            enc(offset,value)
        name = '資料_İ'.encode('utf-16-le') + b'\0\0'
        for j in range(len(name)//2):
            value = struct.unpack_from('<H',name,j*2)[0] ^ ((rol(key,j)+j)&0xffff)
            w16(base+0x60+j*2,value)
        for i,c in enumerate(b'abc\0xyz'):
            data[base+0xc0+i] = c ^ ((rol(key,i)+i)&255)
    return data

def main():
    with work_directory(prefix='vmp-synthetic-') as tmp:
        tmp = Path(tmp)
        for index,(x64,key,named) in enumerate([(True,0,False),(False,0xffffffff,True),(True,0x12345678,True),(False,0x890abcde,False)]):
            src = tmp / f'test-{index}.exe'
            src.write_bytes(image(x64,[(0x137,key,named)]))
            out = tmp / f'out-{index}'
            run(src,'-o',out)
            m = json.loads((out/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            assert m['key'] == hex(key)
            assert m['table_rva'] == '0x1137'
            assert m['architecture'] == ('x64' if x64 else 'x32')
            assert len(m['resources']) == 1
            r = m['resources'][0]
            assert r['type'] == ('資料_İ' if named else 123)
            assert (r['name'],r['language'],r['codepage']) == (543,1041,65001)
            assert (out/r['file']).read_bytes() == b'abc\0xyz'
        src = tmp/'multiple.exe'
        src.write_bytes(image(True,[(0x137,0x12345678,True),(0x737,0xabcd1234,False)]))
        result = run(src,'-o',tmp/'ambiguous',ok=False)
        assert 'Multiple valid tables' in result.stderr
        assert not (tmp/'ambiguous').exists()
        run(src,'--table-rva','0x1737','--key','0xabcd1234','-o',tmp/'explicit')
        broken = image(True,[(0x137,0x12345678,False)])
        struct.pack_into('<I',broken,0x200+0x137+12,0x80000000 ^ 0x12345678)
        src.write_bytes(broken)
        run(src,'--table-rva','0x1137','--key','0x12345678','--list',ok=False)
    print('PASS: PE32/PE32+, Unicode names, arbitrary keys, unaligned tables, ambiguity and cyclic tree rejection')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```
